### `maintenance.summarize_actions_step_timeline` · GitHub Actions 作业/步骤时间线摘要器 / GitHub Actions job/step timeline summarizer

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `9c9981755b58dcfff8dd830d6581c330ea02e191`.
- Can do: Summarize observed job and step timestamp intervals from a caller-supplied GitHub Actions snapshot without network access.
- Good for: Summarizing job and step elapsed intervals from a supplied workflow run/jobs API JSON snapshot bound to one run ID, attempt, and head SHA.; Preserving missing timestamps and distinguishing a complete job list from complete timestamp coverage.; Reviewing observed run/step intervals while keeping overlapping intervals separate from billable minutes or cash cost.
- Not for: Querying GitHub, authenticating the source of a supplied snapshot, or fetching workflow/job records.; Calculating billable runner minutes, service charges, cash cost, user wait, or elapsed workflow duration from overlapping job sums.; Determining required-check satisfaction, test equivalence, CI sufficiency, merge/release authorization, or scientific validity.; Treating equal timestamps on skipped workflow steps as proof that the step executed or consumed measured runtime.
- Use only if: The caller supplies one UTF-8 JSON envelope containing the workflow run and corresponding jobs API snapshot.; Each supplied job binds to the same run ID, attempt, and head SHA declared in workflow_run.; The caller treats snapshot provenance as unauthenticated and accepts that incomplete timestamps produce a partial receipt.
- Do not use when: The task requires live GitHub acquisition or authenticated source provenance.; The requested value is billable time, cash cost, test equivalence, required-check satisfaction, merge permission, or release permission.; The snapshot is confidential and the caller is not authorized to process it in the local repository context.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON stdin envelope with one workflow_run object and a jobs_response containing job snapshots for the same run ID, attempt, and head SHA → Deterministic JSON receipt with observed job and step interval seconds, missing-time markers, snapshot completeness, and input/tool digests
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_CALLER_SUPPLIED_SNAPSHOT` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_actions_step_timeline.py` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Reads bounded JSON from stdin and the tool source for its digest; emits a single JSON receipt to stdout or validation errors to stderr; creates or modifies no files and makes no network request..
- Dependencies: none declared; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · No comparable local latency, memory, or compute-cost profile has been measured; network and billing are disabled.
- Alternatives: `maintenance.inspect_repository` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Generic repository inspection does not parse a caller-supplied Actions jobs snapshot into per-job and per-step intervals; manual API/UI review remains an alternative but has not been compared on the same task.; `maintenance.compare_actions_repeated_step_sequences` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The paired #264 comparison confirms this tool accepts a single run while the batch analyzer requires 2-20 runs and adds cross-run repeated-step grouping; retain the single-run path for isolated snapshots..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Analyze one supplied GitHub Actions run when there is no multi-run comparison set, including its per-job and per-step observed intervals and source-snapshot digest..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · Paired offline analysis of the four-run #264 workflow/event/PR/base history plus the existing one-run API snapshot smoke; confirms distinct cardinalities and output scope, but does not compare latency, CPU, memory, or analyst effort. Comparators: maintenance.compare_actions_repeated_step_sequences; implementation refs: none recorded; refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_actions_step_timeline.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_actions_repeated_step_analysis.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_actions_repeated_step_analysis.py, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-actions-repeated-step-evidence-r0.json; findings: The single-run tool handles one supplied run; the batch tool requires two to twenty runs and emits repeated-step groups. The batch tool cannot replace isolated single-run analysis. Retain both as opt-in specialized tools; broader cost and performance comparisons remain unknown..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · Four-run paired scope evidence shows the single-run input contract remains useful for isolated Actions snapshots, while the batch analyzer covers same-PR run histories.
- Migration/rollback: Do not migrate manual Actions review or registered repository operations based on this single snapshot smoke; preserve the exact caller-supplied input and timestamp-only output contract pending same-task comparison. / Remove this candidate card, wrapper, fixture, test, and scope note from the isolated toolbox branch; regenerate identity cards and TOOLBOX.md; retain the previous commit and evidence..
- Invocation example: `python3 -B ignition/tools/toolbox_actions_step_timeline.py --stdin`
- Fallback: If the envelope is incomplete or inconsistent, stop and report the exact validation error; inspect the source API snapshot manually only when separately authorized, and do not estimate billing from interval totals.
- Evidence: `REAL_TOOL_TEST` · source refs `ignition/tools/toolbox_actions_step_timeline.py` (OFFLINE_STDIN_STDOUT_TOOL), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_actions_step_timeline.py` (LIVE_SNAPSHOT_SMOKE_AND_NEGATIVE_CONTROLS), `ignition/tests/fixtures/toolbox-actions-step-timeline/actions-run-40064357026.json` (MINIMIZED_GITHUB_API_SNAPSHOT_FIXTURE), `ignition/docs/operations/actions-step-timeline-summary.md` (SCOPE_PROVENANCE_AND_CLAIM_CEILING), `ignition/tools/toolbox_actions_repeated_step_analysis.py` (PAIRED_MULTI_RUN_COMPARATOR), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_actions_repeated_step_analysis.py` (PAIRED_MULTI_RUN_COMPARISON_TESTS), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-actions-repeated-step-evidence-r0.json` (PAIRED_MULTI_RUN_COMPARISON_EVIDENCE); focused checks [ignition/tests/test_toolbox_actions_step_timeline.py](tests/test_toolbox_actions_step_timeline.py), [ignition/tests/test_toolbox_routing.py](tests/test_toolbox_routing.py), [ignition/tests/test_toolbox_identity_cards.py](tests/test_toolbox_identity_cards.py); negative controls: Reject mismatched run ID, attempt, or head SHA with nonzero exit and no partial stdout.; Reject duplicate JSON keys, duplicate steps, reversed timestamps, and inputs above 2 MiB.; Preserve missing timestamps as null and mark time coverage partial.; A route proposal is default-excluded and execution_authorized=false; the CLI creates no files and makes no network request..
- Route default: `false` · conditions: Remain excluded unless this exact candidate ID is explicitly allowlisted for a caller-supplied Actions timeline task.; A route proposal does not authenticate input provenance or authorize invocation; no network host is available to this tool..
- Claim ceiling: Observed intervals from caller-supplied unauthenticated JSON only; parallel sums are NOT_BILLABLE_MINUTES and do not establish workflow wall time, hosted runner usage, service charges, test equivalence, required-check status, merge permission, or release authorization. Equal timestamps on skipped steps remain source observations only and do not establish execution or elapsed runtime.

### `research.audit_pubmed_updates_from_doi` · PubMed DOI 更新关联审计 / PubMed DOI update-link audit

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.2.0` · license `Composition wrapper BUSL-1.1; wrapped adapters and NCBI service terms apply independently; no license for provider record data is asserted.` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; no Owner, source-authentication, or scientific authority is implied.` · source blob `8d299026a00a63bd14268a59b35c815019c005ee`.
- Can do: Resolve one caller-supplied DOI through the fixed PMC ID Converter, then read raw PubMed Comments/Corrections only when the exact PMID response also reports the same DOI.
- Good for: A single DOI task that needs both NCBI PMID/PMCID lookup and raw PubMed Comments/Corrections, gated on the exact returned PubMed record reporting the same input DOI.; Returning both source-stage receipts and an explicit skipped outcome when the DOI response contains no exact PMID.; An allowlisted router task whose requested output spans DOI-to-PMID resolution and exact-record update-link fields.
- Not for: Bulk DOI processing; the composition intentionally accepts exactly one DOI per invocation.; Proving that a paper is or is not retracted, corrected, valid, authentic, or scientifically supported.; Treating an unresolved PMID or empty relation list as evidence that no update exists.; Article title, abstract, full text, arbitrary URL, source discovery, or repository-record modification.
- Use only if: The caller supplies one bare DOI and explicitly permits sending it to the fixed public PMC ID Converter; if a PMID resolves, the caller also permits sending that identifier to fixed NCBI EFetch.; The CLI is invoked with --allow-public-network and the environment separately permits both pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov and eutils.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov.; The caller accepts two serial fixed-host GETs at most and the limited meaning of each returned stage.
- Do not use when: The DOI or any derived PMID is confidential or must not be sent to a public service.; Either network host is unavailable, disallowed, or not explicitly approved for this invocation.; The task requires complete retraction status, article-content review, source-authenticity review, or scientific assessment.
- Inputs → outputs: JSON stdin object with exactly one unique bare DOI identifier → One DOI-to-PMID/PMCID identifier response with the provider response digest and explicit acquisition outcome., If one exact PMID is returned, a PubMed relation read gated by an exact DOI match in that same EFetch response; otherwise an explicit no-fetch or RECORD_IDENTITY_NOT_CONFIRMED outcome.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_TWO_HOST_NETWORK_QUERY` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_doi_update_audit.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_update_link_reader.py` · writes `none` · network `true` hosts `pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov, eutils.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: With --allow-public-network, makes one HTTPS GET to pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov using the supplied DOI. It makes at most one subsequent HTTPS GET to eutils.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov and only if the first response yields one exact numeric PMID., Both stages use their existing bounded response, timeout, redirect, content-type, parsing, and no-retry rules; the reader emits relations only when the same EFetch record reports exactly the input DOI. The composition does not widen endpoints or return article title/abstract fields., Reads stdin and writes one JSON result to stdout or an input error to stderr; creates or modifies no repository file and uses no secrets, credentials, API key, billing, or external action..
- Dependencies: ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_update_link_reader.py; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library; the two pinned local adapter modules.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · At most two serial public GET requests. No comparable billed cost, bandwidth, latency distribution, or service-availability benchmark is established.
- Alternatives: `research.resolve_pubmed_identifiers` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): This composition reuses DOI-to-PMID/PMCID resolution but continues to exact PubMed relation fields; the resolver alone does not read Comments/Corrections.; `research.inspect_pubmed_update_links` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The raw relation reader accepts an exact PMID only; this composition starts from one DOI and conditionally feeds it the resolver's returned PMID..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Route one DOI directly to a bounded two-stage resolution and exact PubMed update-link read while preserving independent stage receipts and skipping the second request unless an exact PMID is returned..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · Historical R0 evidence compares separate resolver-plus-reader calls with composition. Version 0.2.0 additionally requires the exact input DOI to appear in the same EFetch record before relations are emitted; one matched read-only task and synthetic identity negative controls support this bounded gate, not broad accuracy, completeness, source authenticity, or replacement. Comparators: research.resolve_pubmed_identifiers, research.inspect_pubmed_update_links; implementation refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_update_link_reader.py; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-c1-evidence-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-c1-evidence-r1.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-real-task-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-real-task-r1.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-route-evidence-r0.json, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_pubmed_doi_update_audit.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_routing.py, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-route-evidence-r1.json; findings: The composition still resolves one DOI and reads relation fields only if one exact PMID is returned.; Version 0.2.0 additionally withholds relations unless the same PubMed EFetch response contains the exact input DOI; the real task records the identity-binding result and response digest.; The reader and composition remain complementary: the reader accepts known PMIDs directly, while the composition accepts one DOI and performs the identifier stage first.; No result proves provider authenticity, exhaustive update coverage, retraction status, or scientific validity; no tool is retired on this evidence..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · Keep as a default-off optional one-DOI two-stage route for tasks needing both identifier resolution and raw PubMed update fields; retain the DOI-only and exact-PMID-only adapters for their own scopes.
- Migration/rollback: Version 0.2.0 preserves the one-DOI route and both existing fixed hosts; if the EFetch response does not bind to the requested DOI, the composition returns RECORD_IDENTITY_NOT_CONFIRMED and withholds relation rows.; No Current registry or default route change is made. Only tasks requiring both DOI resolution and PubMed relations may explicitly allowlist this composition. / Remove the optional card, composition wrapper, focused test, schemas, and human-directory projection from the isolated candidate; regenerate identity projections and preserve source adapter code and historical comparison evidence.; Remove the composition independently; the resolver and exact-PMID reader remain available as before..
- Invocation example: `python3 -B ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_doi_update_audit.py --stdin --allow-public-network`
- Fallback: If the first stage fails or returns no exact PMID, retain its explicit status and do not query EFetch. If the second stage fails or returns no relations, preserve that limited outcome and do not infer absence of an update.
- Evidence: `REAL_TASK_OUTCOME` · source refs `ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_doi_update_audit.py` (BOUNDED_TWO_STAGE_COMPOSITION_CLI), `ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py` (FIXED_HOST_DOI_TO_PUBMED_IDENTIFIER_STAGE), `ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_update_link_reader.py` (FIXED_HOST_EXACT_PMID_RELATION_STAGE), `ignition/schemas/operations/pubmed-doi-update-audit-input-r0.schema.json` (ONE_DOI_INPUT_CONTRACT), `ignition/schemas/operations/pubmed-doi-update-audit-output-r0.schema.json` (STAGED_RESULT_OUTPUT_CONTRACT), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_pubmed_doi_update_audit.py` (FOCUSED_AND_NEGATIVE_CONTROL_TESTS), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-c1-evidence-r0.json` (C1_MATCHED_MANUAL_VS_COMPOSED_TASK_COMPARISON), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-real-task-r0.json` (PRESERVED_PRE_FIX_LIMITED_OUTCOME), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-route-evidence-r0.json` (DEFAULT_OFF_TWO_HOST_ROUTE_EXPERIMENT), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_routing.py` (DEFAULT_OFF_AND_HOST_GATED_ROUTE_NEGATIVE_CONTROLS), `ignition/schemas/operations/pubmed-update-link-inspection-input-r0.schema.json` (COMPOSED_READER_EXPECTED_DOI_BINDING_CONTRACT), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-route-evidence-r1.json` (CANDIDATE_VERSION_0_2_0_DEFAULT_OFF_ROUTE_EXPERIMENT), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-c1-evidence-r1.json` (C1_SAME_TASK_IDENTITY_BINDING_COMPARISON), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-real-task-r1.json` (READ_ONLY_VERSION_0_2_0_REAL_TASK), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-route-evidence-r2.json` (CURRENT_CATALOG_ROUTE_REPLAY_R2); focused checks [ignition/tests/test_toolbox_pubmed_doi_update_audit.py](tests/test_toolbox_pubmed_doi_update_audit.py), [ignition/tests/test_toolbox_routing.py](tests/test_toolbox_routing.py); negative controls: Missing --allow-public-network makes neither stage call.; Reject URL-like, malformed, over-limit, or multi-DOI input before either stage.; Do not call PubMed EFetch when the resolver fails or returns no exact numeric PMID.; Withhold PubMed relations if the matching EFetch record omits the expected DOI, reports a different DOI, reports multiple DOI values, or reports an invalid DOI value.; Keep empty relations and unavailable sources distinct; do not infer no retraction or no update.; Only two pinned fixed hosts are declared; no write, secret, billing, redirect, or arbitrary-URL capability is added..
- Route default: `false` · conditions: Remain excluded unless the candidate is explicitly allowlisted for a one-DOI task that requires both identifier resolution and raw PubMed Comments/Corrections.; The route proposal does not authorize network execution; the actual invocation separately requires --allow-public-network and approval to send the DOI and any returned PMID to the two listed hosts.; Do not select for PMID-only reading, DOI metadata verification, complete retraction adjudication, article-content review, or scientific-claim review..
- Claim ceiling: One live PMC ID Converter DOI-to-PMID response and, only when that response yields an exact PMID, raw Comments/Corrections fields from the matching PubMed record when that same EFetch response reports exactly the input DOI. This identity gate does not authenticate the provider or establish completeness, article content, retraction status, scientific validity, or absence of updates.

### `research.bind_offline_source_snapshot_run` · 离线来源快照绑定 / Offline source-snapshot binding

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `4c1f04686877dd0473b9e635f6019fa2d8362799`.
- Can do: Run one caller-frozen source set through the fixed E37 offline adapter, verify per-capture and canonical source-set digests, and preserve the inputs and run artifacts in a receipt-bound output directory.
- Good for: Replaying one to five saved source captures without search or network access.; Binding each raw capture, the canonical source set, draft, claim-review rows, and output artifacts to explicit SHA-256 values.; Checking exact quote anchors against saved captured text while preserving that semantic support was not evaluated.
- Not for: Finding current sources, refreshing metadata, or retrieving new external material.; Authenticating a publisher or capture origin, deciding whether a quotation supports a claim, or validating a human review judgment.; Establishing scientific truth, study quality, causal support, or publication readiness.
- Use only if: The caller explicitly supplies a UTF-8 saved-source manifest and exact per-capture SHA-256 lock.; The caller supplies the expected canonical source-set SHA-256 before the run; the digest-preview mode can compute it after validating capture locks.; The task needs an offline source-binding receipt and accepts exact-anchor-only validation.; Run output is directed to a new or empty path outside the checked-out repository.
- Do not use when: Current external acquisition or source discovery is required.; The capture origin or publisher authenticity must be independently verified by this tool.; A semantic, scientific, causal, or mathematical judgment is being requested.; The caller cannot provide a trustworthy capture digest lock or safe non-repository output location.
- Inputs → outputs: Caller-supplied offline source-snapshot JSONL manifest with relative capture files, per-capture SHA-256 lock, expected canonical source-set SHA-256, question, draft, claim-review JSONL, task/episode/source-set identifiers, and an output path outside the repository. → A new run directory containing a manifest, receipt, report, normalized sources, claim-anchor ledger, copied review inputs, raw captures, and artifact hashes., Digest-preview JSON containing the verified capture count and canonical source-set SHA-256, written to stdout only.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_REPOSITORY_WITH_CALLER_SELECTED_NONREPOSITORY_RUN_OUTPUT` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_bind_offline_source_snapshot_run.py, ignition/research_workflow/slot.py, ignition/research_workflow/cli.py` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Reads only the caller-selected manifest, its relative capture files, digest lock, draft, and claim-review file plus the fixed local implementation., Run mode creates the caller-selected new/empty output directory and required parents outside the checkout; it rejects symlinks, nonempty destinations, and paths inside or containing the checkout., Digest-preview mode writes one JSON result to stdout and creates no files., No repository files are written; no network, search, model, secret, billing, or external action is used..
- Dependencies: ignition/research_workflow/slot.py, ignition/research_workflow/cli.py; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library, The checked-out ignition.research_workflow.slot and ignition.research_workflow.cli modules.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · The adapter allows up to five source captures of 10 MB each plus a 1 MB manifest. Wall time, memory, and local storage cost have not been calibrated.
- Alternatives: `research.validate_evidence_program_snapshot` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Validates an existing Evidence Program snapshot but does not create this receipt-bound offline source run.; `knowledge.navigate_raw_evidence` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Navigates current repository files by literal terms; it does not bind a caller-supplied external capture manifest and draft.; `research.lookup_openalex_work_metadata` (ALTERNATE_PROVIDER_DIFFERENT_PROVENANCE): Performs an explicitly invoked live OpenAlex metadata lookup; this tool uses saved capture bytes offline..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Offline replay that binds saved external capture bytes, the normalized source set, reviewer-authored claim rows, draft text, and run artifacts into one digest-locked receipt.; Exact quote anchoring against raw captured text when the legacy normalized-record adapter has no capture bytes..

### `research.compare_crossref_reference_metadata` · Crossref 引文元数据比较器 / Crossref Reference Metadata Comparator

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.2.0` · license `Wrapper BUSL-1.1; Crossref bibliographic metadata reuse basis documented at https://www.crossref.org/documentation/retrieve-metadata/.` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; reused Crossref request and Task 103 normalization helpers do not confer Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `764cfd1700d8d76ab9a3157918c8eb7c4d0a392e`.
- Can do: Optionally bind selected Crossref reference keys and DOIs to one source Work, then resolve up to five cited DOI Works and compare the caller-supplied structured fields against both source-deposited and target Work metadata.
- Good for: Resolving one to five caller-selected cited DOI identifiers to their Crossref Work records after the caller supplies the DOI-bearing reference rows.; Comparing only supplied structured article title, year, journal title, volume, issue, and first-page values after the adapter's bounded normalization.; Returning exact DOI-identity status, per-field equality/difference/missing status, response digests, and acquisition failures for each selected row.; Separating source-deposited citation text from metadata on the target Work without claiming that either record is complete or authoritative.; When explicitly requested, confirming that a selected reference key and DOI appear together exactly once in the fetched source Work’s Crossref-deposited reference rows before resolving that target DOI.
- Not for: Authenticating the source Work, publisher provenance, or the origin of a caller-supplied reference key; deciding that a citation is correct or incorrect; or validating scientific support.; Searching or enumerating a bibliography, estimating reference-deposit coverage, or certifying bibliography completeness.; Parsing author names or free-form unstructured citation strings, following citation URLs, reading publisher pages, or downloading article full text.; Making a network call without per-invocation approval of the DOI transmission and the --allow-public-network flag.
- Use only if: The caller has explicitly selected up to five DOI-bearing reference rows and approves their DOI transmission; when verify_source_link is true, the caller also approves sending the source-work DOI to the public Crossref API.; The exact input follows the JSON Schema at ignition/schemas/operations/toolbox-crossref-reference-metadata-comparison-input-r1.schema.json; verify_source_link is an optional boolean defaulting to false.; A routed proposal explicitly includes this candidate and allowed_network_hosts contains exactly api.crossref.org; a route proposal alone is not execution permission.; The actual CLI invocation includes --allow-public-network.
- Do not use when: The DOI or task context is confidential or public-network transmission is not approved.; The caller has only an unstructured citation or needs source provenance, citation correctness, completeness, scientific assessment, or full-text review.; The task requires resolving more than five references or searching for cited works not already selected by the caller.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON object with one source_work_doi and one to five selected reference objects. Each reference has a unique key and bare DOI; optional fields are article_title, year, journal_title, volume, issue, and first_page. Optional verify_source_link is a boolean default false. Maximum 32768 bytes. → Input-ordered per-reference result with bounded source-key/DOI binding status, target DOI identity, acquisition/HTTP state, and selected Crossref Work metadata., When source-link verification is requested, one source Work DOI identity summary plus per-row selected source-reference metadata and supplied-field comparisons; raw source reference lists are not returned., Separate source and target per-field normalized equality/difference/missing statuses, response digests, bounded request-attempt count, and a claim ceiling that excludes citation correctness and scientific support.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_FIXED_HOST_NETWORK_QUERY` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_compare_crossref_reference_metadata.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_crossref_metadata_verifier.py, ignition/evidence-program/tools/run_crossref_verification.py` · writes `none` · network `true` hosts `api.crossref.org` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: For one explicitly invoked run, performs at most five sequential HTTPS GET request attempts for caller-selected cited DOI identifiers using the fixed api.crossref.org host; verify_source_link=true adds one source Work request attempt and prevents target lookup until the source Work DOI and unique source key/DOI binding are checked., Reuses the existing Crossref adapter redirect block, request timeout, response-size cap, and response-body deadline; sends DOI identifiers only, writes a bounded JSON result to stdout, and does not write repository files, follow citation URLs, or retrieve article content..
- Dependencies: ignition/tools/toolbox_crossref_metadata_verifier.py, ignition/evidence-program/tools/run_crossref_verification.py; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library, Local toolbox_crossref_metadata_verifier.py and its SHA-pinned Task 103 Crossref normalization helper.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · No matched latency distribution, bandwidth profile, service availability estimate, or billed-cost measurement exists; the request count and response bounds are explicit.
- Alternatives: `research.verify_crossref_metadata` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Crossref v0.3 can project a bounded set of deposited references from the citing Work response and compare that Work's title/year, but it does not resolve selected cited DOI records or compare their journal, volume, issue, and first page.; `research.compare_publication_metadata_snapshots` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): That tool compares already-collected Crossref/OpenAlex title/year snapshots; it does not retrieve a cited DOI Work or compare source-deposited reference fields against the target record..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Resolve caller-selected DOI-bearing reference rows individually, optionally verify their key/DOI binding against one source Work, and compare supplied structured fields against both the source-deposited row and DOI-resolved target Work while preserving uncertainty..
- Comparison evidence: `COMPARISON_UNKNOWN` · One purposively selected source Work/reference row was checked for exact source DOI identity, unique key and DOI binding, and then resolved against its target Work using two request attempts. Four supplied fields matched the source-deposited row; three matched the target Work and one journal abbreviation/full-title difference remained. This does not estimate general accuracy, coverage, completeness, quality, latency, reliability, or cost. Comparators: research.verify_crossref_metadata, research.compare_publication_metadata_snapshots; implementation refs: none recorded; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-crossref-reference-metadata-comparison-c1-evidence-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-crossref-reference-metadata-comparison-real-task-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-crossref-reference-metadata-comparison-c1-evidence-r1.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-crossref-reference-metadata-comparison-real-task-r1.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-crossref-references-real-task-evidence-r0.json; findings: The source Work response returned the requested DOI and one reference row matched the selected key and target DOI; target lookup proceeded only after that source binding succeeded.; The four supplied fields normalized equal against the source-deposited row. Against the target Work, year, volume, and first page were equal while the abbreviated versus full journal title remained a metadata difference only.; The existing Crossref verifier projects source-deposited references without resolving each selected target Work. The target lookup plus optional source binding is a specialized complement; the single task does not establish a replacement or broad superiority..
- Deprecation/retirement: `ACTIVE` · Optional default-off specialization; one record does not establish broad quality or replacement evidence.
- Migration/rollback: Version 0.2.0 adds an optional source-link verification mode while retaining the default 0.1.0 behavior and the same one-to-five selected-reference limit. No Current operation or route default changes.; Use verify_source_link only when the caller wants a provider-record check of the selected key/DOI pair and approves sending the source-work DOI; use the default mode when only target Work metadata is needed. / Revert the 0.2.0 source-link verification code and input contract to 0.1.0, regenerate identity cards and ignition/TOOLBOX.md, and rerun focused tests. Preserve both real-task records and C1 comparison evidence as history..
- Invocation example: `python3 -B ignition/tools/toolbox_compare_crossref_reference_metadata.py --stdin --allow-public-network`
- Fallback: If a network call is not approved or fails, preserve NOT_RUN or the explicit acquisition failure. Use research.verify_crossref_metadata only when a bounded source-deposited reference projection is sufficient; it does not resolve cited DOI Works.
- Evidence: `REAL_TASK_OUTCOME` · source refs `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-crossref-references-real-task-evidence-r0.json` (SOURCE_WORK_REFERENCE_PROJECTION_AND_SELECTED_INPUT), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-crossref-reference-metadata-comparison-c1-evidence-r0.json` (C1_COMPLEMENTARITY_AND_RETENTION_EVIDENCE), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-crossref-reference-metadata-comparison-real-task-r0.json` (ONE_DOI_REAL_TASK_OUTCOME), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-crossref-reference-metadata-comparison-route-evidence-r0.json` (DEFAULT_OFF_EXACT_HOST_ROUTE_EXPERIMENT), `ignition/docs/operations/toolbox-crossref-reference-metadata-comparison.md` (USER_SCOPE_AND_LIMITS), `ignition/evidence-program/tools/run_crossref_verification.py` (PINNED_TASK103_NORMALIZATION_HELPER), `ignition/schemas/operations/toolbox-crossref-reference-metadata-comparison-input-r0.schema.json` (MACHINE_READABLE_INPUT_CONTRACT), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_crossref_reference_metadata_comparison.py` (FOCUSED_FIELD_DOI_AND_NETWORK_NEGATIVE_CONTROLS), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_routing.py` (EXPLICIT_ALLOWLIST_AND_EXACT_HOST_ROUTE_CONTROLS), `ignition/tools/toolbox_compare_crossref_reference_metadata.py` (FIXED_HOST_SELECTED_REFERENCE_DOI_COMPARATOR), `ignition/tools/toolbox_crossref_metadata_verifier.py` (REUSED_BOUNDED_CROSSREF_HTTP_ADAPTER), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-crossref-reference-metadata-comparison-real-task-r1.json` (SOURCE_BINDING_AND_TARGET_WORK_REAL_TASK_OUTCOME), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-crossref-reference-metadata-comparison-c1-evidence-r1.json` (C1_SOURCE_BINDING_AND_TARGET_COMPLEMENTARITY_EVIDENCE), `ignition/schemas/operations/toolbox-crossref-reference-metadata-comparison-input-r1.schema.json` (MACHINE_READABLE_INPUT_CONTRACT_R1), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-crossref-reference-metadata-comparison-route-evidence-r1.json` (CURRENT_CATALOG_ROUTE_REPLAY_R1); focused checks [ignition/tests/test_toolbox_crossref_reference_metadata_comparison.py](tests/test_toolbox_crossref_reference_metadata_comparison.py), [ignition/tests/test_toolbox_identity_cards.py](tests/test_toolbox_identity_cards.py), [ignition/tests/test_toolbox_routing.py](tests/test_toolbox_routing.py); Reject or mark malformed provider JSON, duplicate provider keys, unavailable DOI identity, and returned DOI mismatch without comparing citation fields.; Keep provider-field absence distinct from normalized differences and report selected fields only.; Call only the fixed api.crossref.org host, follow no redirects, write no repository files, and retrieve no citation URLs or article content.; Keep the candidate default-off; route requires explicit inclusion plus exact api.crossref.org, and planner output cannot authorize execution.; When verify_source_link is true, skip target requests if the source Work DOI is absent/different, the source reference key is missing/ambiguous/malformed, or the source row DOI differs from the selected DOI.; - Claim ceiling: For up to five caller-selected Crossref reference rows, report normalized supplied-field equality or difference against the DOI-resolved target Work. ### `research.compare_publication_metadata_snapshots` · 跨来源出版元数据比较器 / Cross-source publication metadata comparator

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `Runtime code: BUSL-1.1 under LICENSE; input schema: Apache-2.0 under LICENSE; documentation: CC-BY-NC-SA-4.0. Provider metadata keeps its source rights.` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; no source-authentication, provider-authority, or scientific-review responsibility is implied.` · source blob `367170e3b8104f212a0b63d7689a268b47b943ad`.
- Can do: Compares title and publication year in one caller-supplied Crossref verifier result and one OpenAlex lookup result for the same DOI. It reports field-level agreement, disagreement, or insufficiency without choosing a preferred provider.
- Good for: Comparing the reported title and publication year from the existing Crossref and OpenAlex adapters for the same bare DOI.; Producing a field-by-field AGREE, DISAGREE, or INSUFFICIENT result from already collected provider outputs without another source request.; Surfacing a metadata conflict for a researcher to inspect while retaining each adapter-provided response digest.
- Not for: Fetching provider data, authenticating adapter outputs or provider responses, or proving that either result is genuine.; Resolving publication identity from title similarity, punctuation differences, authors, venue, or article contents.; Selecting a trusted or preferred provider, deciding which metadata value is correct, or validating scientific claims.; Checking retraction or correction status; the two adapters return distinct provider-specific status signals that this tool does not reconcile.
- Use only if: Both complete result objects came from the supported 0.1.0 Crossref and OpenAlex adapters for one identical DOI.; The caller specifically needs title/year comparison after source acquisition; the source calls themselves require separate permission and routing.; A local, read-only comparison with no additional network access is appropriate.
- Do not use when: Either source result is missing, belongs to another DOI, or uses an unsupported adapter schema/version.; The task requires verified source authenticity, authoritative publication identity, article content review, or a complete editorial-status judgment.; The DOI must not be included in a local comparison record or the caller cannot disclose source metadata to the process.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON stdin up to 262144 bytes with one DOI and exactly one full Crossref verifier result plus one full OpenAlex lookup result, both from adapter version 0.1.0. → Field-level title and publication-year AGREE, DISAGREE, or INSUFFICIENT status with the observed values per adapter., Per-provider acquisition outcome and response SHA-256 copied from each adapter result; no raw provider response or article content.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `LOCAL_READ_ONLY_STDIN_STDOUT` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `none` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Reads the bounded caller-supplied JSON from stdin and writes one JSON result to stdout or an error to stderr., No filesystem, network, secret, billing, repository-write, or external-action access..
- Dependencies: none declared; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library; no third-party runtime dependency..
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · No stable latency, memory, or cost benchmark has been measured; the tool makes no provider call.
- Alternatives: `research.verify_crossref_metadata` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Crossref retrieves one provider record and compares it with caller-supplied expected values; this tool compares its result with an OpenAlex result and does not fetch data.; `research.lookup_openalex_work_metadata` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): OpenAlex returns Work metadata; this tool compares its selected fields with an already collected Crossref result and does not fetch data..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Create an explicit field-level comparison across the two supported provider result objects after retrieval, without adding another network request or selecting a preferred source..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · One live GAP001-05 DOI task compared the current Crossref and OpenAlex outputs; a matched changed-year negative control verifies conflict reporting. This demonstrates a specialized local comparison output, not provider quality, broad reliability, latency, or cost. Comparators: research.verify_crossref_metadata, research.lookup_openalex_work_metadata; implementation refs: none recorded; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-publication-metadata-cross-source-c1-evidence-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-publication-metadata-cross-source-real-task-r0.json; findings: Both source adapters return title/year observations and response digests but do not emit a combined field-level agreement/disagreement report.; For the live GAP001-05 task both providers returned the same title and year; the comparator reported agreement and preserved both response digests.; A matched synthetic year conflict produced DISAGREE without selecting a preferred provider; there is no basis to retire or replace either source adapter..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · Retain as an optional local post-processor for explicit Crossref/OpenAlex title/year comparison. It neither retrieves data nor covers the source-specific uses of either adapter.
- Migration/rollback: No Current operation or adapter migration is introduced. Callers must first collect the two source results under their own task and permission decisions. / Remove this optional card, local comparator, schema, focused test, documentation, and associated evidence from the candidate; regenerate the identity-card and human toolbox projections. Preserve the source-adapter tools and their evidence..
- Invocation example: `python3 -B ignition/tools/toolbox_compare_publication_metadata.py --stdin`
- Fallback: If either adapter result is missing, failed, mismatched to the DOI, or outside the supported schema/version, preserve INSUFFICIENT or the explicit input error and inspect each source result independently; do not infer agreement.
- Evidence: `REAL_TASK_OUTCOME` · source refs `ignition/tools/toolbox_compare_publication_metadata.py` (LOCAL_COMPARISON_IMPLEMENTATION), `ignition/schemas/operations/publication-metadata-comparison-input-r0.schema.json` (BOUNDED_INPUT_CONTRACT), `ignition/schemas/operations/publication-metadata-comparison-output-r0.schema.json` (MACHINE_READABLE_OUTPUT_CONTRACT), `ignition/data/external-research/104-source-registry.jsonl` (GAP001-05_SOURCE_RECORD_FOR_MATCHED_TASK), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-openalex-live-task-evidence-r0.json` (PRIOR_PAIRED_CROSSREF_OPENALEX_TASK_CONTEXT), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_publication_metadata_comparison.py` (FOCUSED_AND_NEGATIVE_CONTROL_TESTS), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-publication-metadata-cross-source-real-input-r0.json` (REPRODUCIBLE_LIVE_ADAPTER_OUTPUT_INPUT), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-publication-metadata-cross-source-c1-evidence-r0.json` (C1_MATCHED_COMPARISON_AND_LIMITS), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-publication-metadata-cross-source-route-evidence-r0.json` (DEFAULT_OFF_AND_OPT_IN_ROUTE_EXPERIMENT), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-publication-metadata-cross-source-real-task-r0.json` (LIVE_SOURCE_PAIR_AND_COMPARISON_OUTCOME), `ignition/docs/operations/toolbox-publication-metadata-cross-source-comparison.md` (HUMAN_CONTRACT_AND_LIMITS); focused checks [ignition/tests/test_toolbox_publication_metadata_comparison.py](tests/test_toolbox_publication_metadata_comparison.py), [ignition/tests/test_toolbox_routing.py](tests/test_toolbox_routing.py); negative controls: Reject duplicate JSON keys, non-standard numbers, extra envelope properties, oversized input, malformed DOI, unsupported adapter identity/version, invalid response digests, and source results for another DOI.; A missing provider field or explicit acquisition failure remains INSUFFICIENT and is never represented as agreement.; A changed source title or publication year yields DISAGREE without choosing which provider is correct.; The comparator has no network or file access and cannot fetch missing source data..
- Route default: `false` · conditions: Remain excluded unless explicitly allowlisted for a task that already has both supported source-result objects and specifically requires title/year comparison.; The route planner only proposes this local comparison; it does not authorize the prior public Crossref or OpenAlex calls.; Do not route for a single-provider lookup, source authenticity, article-content, retraction, or scientific-claim adjudication..
- Claim ceiling: Agreement or disagreement in title/year fields returned by caller-supplied Crossref and OpenAlex result objects only. The comparator does not authenticate inputs or providers, establish publication identity, verify article contents, decide scientific truth, or reconcile retraction status.

### `research.inspect_pubmed_update_links` · PubMed 更新关联读取器 / PubMed update-link reader

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.2.0` · license `Wrapper BUSL-1.1; NCBI E-utilities usage terms apply, and no license for underlying PubMed record data is asserted. API documentation: https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/books/NBK25499/.` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `fea4231a4879c609b02c16547174aadcbd403024`.
- Can do: Read raw Comments/Corrections relations for exact PubMed PMIDs from fixed NCBI EFetch; an optional expected DOI is checked against the same returned record before relations are emitted.
- Good for: Reading raw PubMed Comments/Corrections RefType, citation text, and linked PMID fields for one or more already-known PubMed records.; Recording whether a specific PubMed response contains relation rows without interpreting the relation as a full scientific or editorial adjudication.; Reviewing a known PubMed record after a caller has obtained its PMID directly or through a separately selected identifier resolver.
- Not for: Retrieving, displaying, or reviewing article titles, abstracts, full text, or arbitrary publisher content; the upstream XML may contain an abstract but this wrapper discards it.; Proving that a paper is or is not retracted, corrected, valid, authentic, or scientifically supported.; Treating an empty or missing Comments/Corrections list as evidence that no update exists.; Searching for PMIDs from arbitrary text or changing a repository research record.
- Use only if: The caller supplies one to ten exact numeric PubMed IDs and explicitly permits sending those identifiers to NCBI for this invocation.; The caller invokes the CLI with --allow-public-network.; The exact host eutils.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov is permitted by the actual invocation environment; a route proposal alone is not authority.; The caller understands that only the update links present in the returned record are reported.
- Do not use when: The PMID or its presence in task context is confidential or must not be sent to a public service.; Network access is unavailable, disallowed, or not explicitly approved for this invocation.; The task requires complete retraction status, article-content review, source-authenticity review, or scientific assessment.
- Inputs → outputs: JSON stdin object with one to ten unique numeric PubMed IDs (PMIDs); optional expected_doi is accepted only for exactly one PMID and must be a bare DOI. → input-ordered PubMed Comments/Corrections relation rows with raw RefType, related PMID, and reference source, when expected_doi is supplied: expected DOI, same-response DOI binding status, and relations only on EXACT_MATCH, per-response SHA-256 and explicit HTTP or network outcome summary
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_FIXED_HOST_NETWORK_QUERY` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_update_link_reader.py` · writes `none` · network `true` hosts `eutils.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: When explicitly invoked with --allow-public-network, sends at most ten HTTPS GET requests to https://eutils.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/entrez/eutils/efetch.fcgi with the supplied PMID and fixed PubMed/XML/tool parameters; optional expected_doi is used only for local response validation and is not sent to NCBI., The upstream XML response may contain a full PubMed record including an abstract; the wrapper parses only the exact PMID, DOI identifiers when requested, and CommentsCorrectionsList, discards the rest, emits no abstract/title, writes only JSON to stdout, and creates or modifies no repository files., Uses no redirects, secrets, API key, billing, retry, or arbitrary URL; applies a five-second socket timeout, seven-second response-body budget with bounded 8 KiB reads, 256 KiB response cap, and 0.34-second serial inter-request delay..
- Dependencies: none declared; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · No comparable latency, bandwidth, service-availability, or compute-cost profile has been measured; the wrapper allows at most ten serial bounded PubMed XML requests.
- Alternatives: `research.resolve_pubmed_identifiers` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The identifier resolver maps DOI values to returned PMID/PMCID values; this tool requires an exact PMID and reads only the record's raw comments/corrections relations.; `research.audit_pubmed_updates_from_doi` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The composition starts from one DOI and performs lookup conditionally; this standalone reader remains useful when the caller already has an exact PMID and needs only its raw relation fields..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Read only the raw Comments/Corrections fields for exact caller-supplied PMIDs, including PMIDs obtained outside the DOI resolver.; Avoid an unnecessary DOI-conversion request when the task already has an authorized exact PMID..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · Historical R0 evidence compares a known-PMID read as a separate stage and inside DOI composition. R1 adds a same-response expected-DOI identity gate; synthetic negative controls withhold relations for mismatch, absence, multiple identifiers, and malformed identifier values. No replacement or broad quality superiority is established. Comparators: research.resolve_pubmed_identifiers, research.audit_pubmed_updates_from_doi; implementation refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_doi_update_audit.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-c1-evidence-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-c1-evidence-r1.json, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_pubmed_update_link_reader.py, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-route-evidence-r1.json; findings: The standalone reader retains exact-PMID tasks, including caller-supplied PMIDs that do not originate from a DOI resolver.; When a composing task supplies expected_doi, the reader returns relations only after the same matching PubMed response contains exactly that DOI; negative cases return no relations.; Identity binding improves attribution of the returned record to the requested DOI but does not authenticate the source or establish comprehensive update detection.; The exact-PMID contract remains a unique standalone use case; no reader retirement or replacement is supported..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · Keep the default-off exact-PMID reader for caller-supplied PMID tasks; the DOI composition extends the chain but does not cover this standalone input scope.
- Migration/rollback: Version 0.2.0 remains backward-compatible for PMID-only inputs; an optional expected_doi adds a same-response identity gate and is restricted to one PMID.; Existing PMID-only calls remain on this adapter and incur no identifier-resolution request or DOI validation. / Remove the candidate entry and wrapper from the isolated toolbox branch, regenerate the identity and human-directory projections, and preserve prior commits and evidence.; If the composition is removed, keep this standalone exact-PMID reader and its prior evidence untouched..
- Invocation example: `python3 -B ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_update_link_reader.py --stdin --allow-public-network`
- Fallback: If live network use is not approved or fails, preserve NOT_RUN or the acquisition failure and open the official PubMed record manually only when the caller separately authorizes that lookup; never infer no update from an empty/unavailable response.
- Evidence: `REAL_TASK_OUTCOME` · source refs `ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_update_link_reader.py` (BOUNDED_FIXED_HOST_CLI), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_pubmed_update_link_reader.py` (FOCUSED_MOCKED_NETWORK_AND_NEGATIVE_CONTROLS), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-c1-evidence-r0.json` (C1_MATCHED_COMPOSITION_COMPARISON), `ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_doi_update_audit.py` (C1_COMPOSED_ROUTE_IMPLEMENTATION_COMPARATOR), `ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py` (C1_DOI_IDENTIFIER_STAGE_IMPLEMENTATION_COMPARATOR), `ignition/schemas/operations/pubmed-update-link-inspection-input-r0.schema.json` (TYPED_PMID_AND_OPTIONAL_EXPECTED_DOI_INPUT_CONTRACT), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-route-evidence-r1.json` (CANDIDATE_VERSION_0_2_0_DEFAULT_OFF_ROUTE_EXPERIMENT), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-c1-evidence-r1.json` (C1_SAME_TASK_IDENTITY_BINDING_COMPARISON), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-route-evidence-r2.json` (CURRENT_CATALOG_ROUTE_REPLAY_R2); focused checks [ignition/tests/test_toolbox_pubmed_update_link_reader.py](tests/test_toolbox_pubmed_update_link_reader.py); negative controls: Missing --allow-public-network makes no request and emits no result.; Reject malformed, duplicate, nonnumeric, URL-like, and over-limit PMIDs before network access.; An optional expected_doi requires exactly one PMID and a bare DOI; absent, malformed, mismatched, multiple, or invalid DOI identifiers in that same EFetch record return no relations.; Only eutils.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov is contacted; redirects, non-XML, oversized responses, HTTP errors, timeout, malformed XML, entity declarations, and mismatched PMID records remain explicit outcomes.; Return raw relation fields without article titles/abstracts; empty relation lists remain limited observations and no repository files are written..
- Route default: `false` · conditions: Remain excluded unless the candidate ID is explicitly allowlisted and the typed route profile explicitly lists eutils.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov in allowed_network_hosts.; A route proposal has execution_authorized=false and does not validate the caller authority behind that host list.; Every actual CLI invocation separately requires --allow-public-network and a task-specific decision to transmit the supplied PMID values..
- Claim ceiling: Raw PubMed Comments/Corrections fields for exact records at retrieval time only. When an optional expected DOI is supplied, relations are emitted only if that same EFetch record reports exactly one valid matching DOI; this binds response identity but does not authenticate the provider source. The response may omit relationships that exist elsewhere or have not been added, so no completeness, retraction-status, content, authenticity, scientific-validity, or absence claim is established.

### `research.lookup_arxiv_paper_metadata` · arXiv 论文元数据查询器 / arXiv paper metadata lookup

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `Wrapper BUSL-1.1; arXiv metadata source and reuse terms remain provider-controlled.` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; no Owner, source-authentication, or scientific authority.` · source blob `fb932d94028e4a75c2e2e97eba2391d9d4ebd78d`.
- Can do: Look up selected current arXiv metadata for one caller-supplied modern arXiv identifier using one bounded fixed-host HTTPS request.
- Good for: Resolving one modern arXiv identifier to its provider-reported current entry/version, title, authors, dates, categories, journal reference, and DOI when supplied.; Checking an arXiv citation identifier before literature work while keeping source metadata retrieval separate from claim support and scientific judgment.; Retaining the request URL, retrieval timestamp, response digest, and explicit acquisition or parse status without storing the provider response.
- Not for: Retrieving abstracts, article files, full text, or arbitrary URLs.; Establishing that an article is authentic, supports a claim, is scientifically valid, peer reviewed, or accepted by a venue.; Resolving DOI identifiers, legacy slash-form arXiv identifiers, or provider records outside the modern arXiv identifier format.; Sending confidential task identifiers or metadata to a public service without caller approval.
- Use only if: The caller explicitly approves sending one modern arXiv identifier to the public arXiv API and passes --allow-public-network.; A route proposal includes this exact candidate ID and export.arxiv.org in allowed_network_hosts; a proposal does not authorize execution.; The identifier matches YYMM.NNNN[N] with an optional lowercase vN suffix and the caller accepts unauthenticated provider metadata.; The fixed HTTPS arXiv API is reachable from the declared environment.
- Do not use when: The identifier or task context must remain private and cannot be transmitted to arXiv.; Network access is disallowed, unavailable, or not approved for this invocation.; The task needs full text, an abstract, source authenticity, claim entailment, scientific assessment, or a DOI-based provider comparison.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON stdin object with exactly one modern arXiv ID (YYMM.NNNN[N], optionally vN) → Provider-reported arXiv entry ID/version, title, author names, dates, categories, journal reference, and DOI when present, One request URL, retrieval timestamp, response SHA-256, byte count, and claim-ceiling receipt
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_FIXED_HOST_NETWORK_QUERY` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_arxiv_metadata_lookup.py` · writes `none` · network `true` hosts `export.arxiv.org` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: When explicitly invoked with --allow-public-network, sends one fixed-host HTTPS GET to export.arxiv.org containing only the caller-supplied arXiv ID; it does not send title text or arbitrary query parameters., Reads bounded UTF-8 JSON and emits one selected metadata receipt to stdout or a validation error to stderr; it creates or modifies no files and retains no raw response., Rejects redirects, non-Atom content, oversized or slow responses, malformed XML, DTD/entity payloads, error entries, and mismatched provider IDs as explicit statuses..
- Dependencies: none declared; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · One bounded public metadata request was observed; comparable latency, reliability, bandwidth, or service-cost profiling has not been done.
- Alternatives: `research.verify_crossref_metadata` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Crossref compares caller-provided expected title/year by bare DOI; the arXiv adapter accepts a modern arXiv ID and returns the arXiv provider entry/version. The identifiers and output contracts differ.; `research.lookup_openalex_work_metadata` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): OpenAlex requires a DOI and returns OpenAlex Work fields; the arXiv adapter directly resolves the source arXiv ID and provider entry/version without requiring a DOI..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Resolve a cited modern arXiv ID directly, including the provider entry/version when no DOI is supplied; DOI-based Crossref and OpenAlex candidates cannot consume that identifier as input..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · One real arXiv API lookup for the arXiv ID cited by Workbench #81 returned an arXiv entry/version and no DOI. Current Crossref and OpenAlex adapters require bare DOI inputs. This establishes a distinct identifier contract for that task, not comparative accuracy, coverage beyond this sample, performance, effort, or cost. Comparators: research.verify_crossref_metadata, research.lookup_openalex_work_metadata; implementation refs: none recorded; refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_arxiv_metadata_lookup.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_arxiv_metadata_lookup.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_routing.py, ignition/docs/operations/arxiv-metadata-lookup.md, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-arxiv-metadata-live-task-evidence-r0.json, ignition/tools/toolbox_crossref_metadata_verifier.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_openalex_work_metadata.py; findings: For the source-bound Workbench #81 cited ID 2610.04074, the live arXiv API returned entry 2610.04074v2 and no DOI. Existing Crossref/OpenAlex adapters accept bare DOI inputs, so they cannot directly resolve this input. This demonstrates specialized identifier coverage; it does not establish a broader provider or performance advantage..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · The live source-bound task returned an arXiv entry/version without a DOI; direct modern arXiv-ID lookup is outside the input contracts of the current DOI-based metadata tools.
- Migration/rollback: Keep this tool opt-in for direct arXiv identifiers. When an arXiv response includes a DOI and DOI-provider fields are specifically required, route that separate task to Crossref or OpenAlex as well. No Current operation or default route changes. / Remove this optional candidate, tool, focused tests, evidence, and scope note from the isolated branch; regenerate the candidate identity cards and TOOLBOX.md; retain the source API URL and recorded evidence if useful..
- Invocation example: `python3 -B ignition/tools/toolbox_arxiv_metadata_lookup.py --stdin --allow-public-network`
- Fallback: If live lookup is not approved or fails, retain the explicit acquisition outcome and use the official arXiv abstract page only when separately authorized; do not infer that a failed API lookup means the paper does not exist.
- Evidence: `REAL_TASK_OUTCOME` · source refs `ignition/tools/toolbox_arxiv_metadata_lookup.py` (BOUNDED_FIXED_HOST_METADATA_LOOKUP), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_arxiv_metadata_lookup.py` (MOCKED_NETWORK_AND_NEGATIVE_CONTROLS), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_routing.py` (DEFAULT_OFF_AND_EXACT_HOST_ROUTING_TEST), `ignition/docs/operations/arxiv-metadata-lookup.md` (API_SCOPE_AND_BLUEPRINT_PROVENANCE), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-arxiv-metadata-live-task-evidence-r0.json` (LIVE_API_TASK_AND_ROUTE_EVIDENCE), `ignition/tools/toolbox_crossref_metadata_verifier.py` (DOI_INPUT_COMPARATOR), `ignition/tools/toolbox_openalex_work_metadata.py` (DOI_INPUT_COMPARATOR); focused checks [ignition/tests/test_toolbox_arxiv_metadata_lookup.py](tests/test_toolbox_arxiv_metadata_lookup.py), [ignition/tests/test_toolbox_routing.py](tests/test_toolbox_routing.py), [ignition/tests/test_toolbox_identity_cards.py](tests/test_toolbox_identity_cards.py); negative controls: Missing --allow-public-network performs no request and emits no result.; Reject DOI/URL inputs, legacy slash-form IDs, invalid months or versions, duplicate JSON keys, and requests above 4 KiB before network access.; Only export.arxiv.org receives a request; redirects, non-Atom content, oversized or slow bodies, DTD/entities, malformed XML, provider errors, and mismatched record IDs return explicit non-success outcomes.; The receipt excludes abstracts/full text and does not authenticate source identity or scientific claims; invocation creates no files..
- Route default: `false` · conditions: Remain excluded unless this exact candidate ID is explicitly allowlisted for a direct modern arXiv-ID metadata task.; Require the route profile to list export.arxiv.org explicitly; the route planner does not verify caller authority or grant network permission.; Every actual invocation also requires --allow-public-network and a task-specific decision to transmit the identifier.; Do not select this tool only to repeat DOI metadata or infer article support..
- Claim ceiling: Provider-reported selected arXiv metadata for one caller-supplied modern ID only; the result does not authenticate a source, retrieve article content, establish claim support, or imply scientific validity or acceptance.

### `research.lookup_noaa_global_ch4_annual_growth` · NOAA 全球甲烷年度增量读取器 / NOAA global methane annual growth lookup

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `Wrapper BUSL-1.1; NOAA GML source-use page requests fair credit for its measurements and data.` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field confers no NOAA, Owner, emissions-accounting, or scientific authority.` · source blob `138e5eb50953a19d5c83455f063a3fc7b0a7badd`.
- Can do: Retrieve a bounded year range from NOAA GML’s fixed global annual atmospheric CH4 mole-fraction increment table, including provider-reported uncertainty.
- Good for: Retrieving NOAA GML global annual atmospheric methane mole-fraction increment and uncertainty for an explicit bounded year range.; Recording provider response digest, HTTP metadata, retrieval time, and explicit missing-year or acquisition status for source review.
- Not for: Estimating methane emissions, identifying emitting sectors, attributing causes, or making a scientific conclusion.; Retrieving arbitrary URLs, station-level/monthly data, article content, or unrestricted date ranges.; Treating a missing year as zero, interpolating values, or declaring a provider failure to prove that data do not exist.
- Use only if: The caller has decided that the public NOAA GML request is appropriate and passes --allow-public-network for this invocation.; The request contains exactly integer start_year and end_year values from 1984 through 2100, with no more than 50 inclusive years.; A proposed route explicitly includes this candidate ID and allowed_network_hosts contains exactly gml.noaa.gov; route planning is not execution authorization.
- Do not use when: Public network access is disallowed or the caller must not contact NOAA GML.; The task needs a specific greenhouse-gas inventory, source attribution, causal explanation, station data, or independent method validation.; The requested range exceeds the tool’s year or size bounds, or a precise historical snapshot is required and the live response is not archived.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON stdin object with exactly integer start_year and end_year (1984–2100 inclusive; range at most 50 years) → Provider-reported annual CH4 atmospheric mole-fraction increment and uncertainty in ppb for each returned year, with explicit FOUND, PARTIAL_DATA, or NO_MATCH status, Fixed NOAA source URL, retrieval timestamp, HTTP status, ETag/Last-Modified when supplied, response SHA-256, and source caveats
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_FIXED_HOST_NETWORK_QUERY` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_noaa_ch4_annual_growth.py` · writes `none` · network `true` hosts `gml.noaa.gov` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: When explicitly invoked with --allow-public-network, sends one HTTPS GET to the fixed NOAA GML table URL; year bounds are applied locally and no caller URL or private identifier is transmitted., Writes a bounded selected-record JSON receipt to stdout and errors to stderr; does not retain the raw response or write repository files., Rejects redirects, non-text content, oversized or slow responses, malformed headers/tables, duplicate/out-of-order years, and invalid numeric rows as explicit outcomes..
- Dependencies: none declared; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · One bounded public GET is permitted per invocation; no comparable latency, reliability, bandwidth, or compute-cost profile has been measured.
- Alternatives: `research.verify_crossref_metadata` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Crossref compares DOI-keyed bibliographic metadata and does not retrieve atmospheric CH4 annual growth observations.; `research.lookup_openalex_work_metadata` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): OpenAlex returns DOI-keyed scholarly Work metadata and does not retrieve atmospheric CH4 annual growth observations.; `research.lookup_noaa_global_co2_annual_growth` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The CO2 adapter reads a separate NOAA table and returns ppm; it cannot replace this methane/ppb task contract..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Retrieve a year-bounded global atmospheric CH4 annual mole-fraction increment and uncertainty series directly from NOAA GML; the current DOI metadata adapters do not accept this data request or return this observation type..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · Function and contract comparison against the DOI-keyed Crossref/OpenAlex bibliographic adapters and the complementary NOAA GML global CO2 annual-growth adapter. The NOAA tools return different gas-specific observations and units; the bibliographic tools do not return atmospheric observations. This establishes distinct use by task, not comparative reliability, latency, quality, or cost. Comparators: research.verify_crossref_metadata, research.lookup_openalex_work_metadata, research.lookup_noaa_global_co2_annual_growth; implementation refs: none recorded; refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_noaa_ch4_annual_growth.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_crossref_metadata_verifier.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_openalex_work_metadata.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_noaa_ch4_annual_growth.py, ignition/docs/operations/noaa-ch4-annual-growth-lookup.md, ignition/tools/toolbox_noaa_co2_annual_growth.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_noaa_co2_annual_growth.py, ignition/docs/operations/noaa-co2-annual-growth-lookup.md; findings: The existing CH4 adapter returns annual atmospheric methane increments and uncertainty in ppb. The CO2 adapter reads a different NOAA table and returns carbon-dioxide increments in ppm; neither gas-specific tool can satisfy the other species-specific request.; Crossref and OpenAlex are DOI-keyed bibliographic metadata tools and do not return either atmospheric observation series.; Retain the CH4 and CO2 adapters as separate default-off tools by task. This comparison establishes contract complementarity, not performance, source agreement, or scientific validity; cost remains unknown..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · Retain as an optional, default-off source-specific adapter for the distinct NOAA atmospheric annual-growth contract; it does not replace the compared bibliographic metadata tools.
- Migration/rollback: No Current registry or default route migration is made. If a future NOAA adapter overlaps this contract, compare identical year ranges and output fields before changing either disposition. / Remove the optional catalog entry, wrapper, tests, and scope note from the isolated branch; regenerate the candidate identity-card and TOOLBOX.md projections; retain the historical source snapshot and comparison rationale if still useful..

- Fallback: If public network use is not approved or retrieval fails, report the explicit NOT_RUN or acquisition status and consult an appropriately pinned NOAA snapshot. Never label a cached snapshot as a live check or convert missing rows to zero.

- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · Function and contract comparison against the existing NOAA GML global CH4 annual growth adapter and the DOI-keyed Crossref/OpenAlex adapters. CO2 and CH4 are distinct species with different fixed source tables and units; neither gas-specific adapter supplies the other gas. This supports coexistence by task, not comparative reliability, latency, quality, or cost. Comparators: research.lookup_noaa_global_ch4_annual_growth, research.verify_crossref_metadata, research.lookup_openalex_work_metadata; implementation refs: none recorded; refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_noaa_co2_annual_growth.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_noaa_co2_annual_growth.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_noaa_ch4_annual_growth.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_noaa_ch4_annual_growth.py, ignition/docs/operations/noaa-co2-annual-growth-lookup.md, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_routing.py, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-noaa-co2-source-review-r0.json; findings: The NOAA CO2 table returns carbon-dioxide annual increments and uncertainty in ppm; the existing CH4 table returns methane increments in ppb. Their input/output units and fixed paths are species-specific, so both tools remain useful for different research questions.; The CH4 tool cannot satisfy a CO2/ppm request; Crossref and OpenAlex are bibliographic metadata tools rather than atmospheric observation readers.; No performance or cost superiority is claimed; live service quality and comparable cost remain unknown..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · Retain as a separate optional default-off NOAA CO2 table reader. It complements, and does not replace, the CH4 table reader because the species, source path, and units differ.
- Migration/rollback: No Current registry or default route migration is made. Keep the CH4 adapter for CH4/ppb tasks and explicitly select this candidate only for CO2/ppm tasks. / Remove this optional catalog entry, wrapper, tests, route receipt, and scope note from the isolated branch; regenerate candidate identity cards and TOOLBOX.md; preserve the evidence showing why the CO2 and CH4 contracts are distinct if still useful..
