### `research.lookup_openalex_work_metadata` · OpenAlex Work 元数据查询器 / OpenAlex Work metadata lookup

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.2.0` · license `Wrapper BUSL-1.1. OpenAlex states its metadata is CC0: https://help.openalex.org/data/how-its-built/. Public API usage is metered: https://help.openalex.org/api/authentication/ and https://help.openalex.org/access/example-costs/.` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field grants no Owner, retraction, or scientific authority.` · source blob `3aaad5f911bd0f7f44aeb0c6e08389cf5de12058`.
- Can do: Retrieve a bounded exact-primary-DOI-matched OpenAlex Work projection, including provider-reported open-access and location metadata.
- Good for: Retrieving an exact-primary-DOI-matched OpenAlex Work ID, title, publication year, work type, and the API-reported is_retracted flag.; Returning OpenAlex-reported open_access, best_oa_location, and up to 25 sanitized location records with an explicit count and truncation flag.; Recording a bounded OpenAlex response digest, HTTP result, and server-reported rate-limit and usage fields without retaining the raw response.; Adding OpenAlex-specific work identity, location metadata, and retraction-signal output when a task explicitly requests those provider fields alongside another source.
- Not for: Retrieving abstracts, article full text, publisher pages, or arbitrary URLs; returned location URLs are never requested.; Establishing source authenticity, scientific truth, article-content support, URL availability, legal reuse rights, or complete OpenAlex coverage.; Treating OpenAlex is_retracted as an authoritative or complete retraction adjudication; the documented signal is sourced from Retraction Watch.; Treating no exact primary DOI match as proof that a work does not exist; DOI filters can match DOI values on work locations.; Selecting a best guess when multiple exact primary DOI matches are returned or updating repository source records.
- Use only if: The caller explicitly permits transmitting the supplied public DOI identifiers for this invocation and passes --allow-public-network.; The invocation uses only the fixed HTTPS host api.openalex.org and a JSON stdin object with one to five unique bare DOI identifiers.; A route proposal may include this candidate only when the task explicitly requires OpenAlex Work identity, location, or other provider-reported fields and allowed_network_hosts contains api.openalex.org.; The route proposal remains non-authoritative; actual invocation consent is checked separately by the CLI flag and task-specific decision.
- Do not use when: A DOI is confidential or must not be transmitted to a public scholarly metadata service.; Network access is unavailable, disallowed, or not explicitly approved for this invocation.; The task needs an authoritative or complete retraction decision, article-content review, URL access verification, reuse-rights determination, or scientific assessment.; The task requires an exhaustive search, an unbounded query, cursor paging, or full raw-response retention.
- Inputs → outputs: JSON stdin object with one to five unique bare DOI identifiers → input-ordered OpenAlex Work ID and selected bibliographic metadata rows including the provider-reported is_retracted signal, OpenAlex-reported open-access summary, best OA location, and up to 25 sanitized location records per matched Work; provider count and truncation state remain explicit, per-request response SHA-256, HTTP status, and public API usage fields
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_FIXED_HOST_NETWORK_QUERY` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_openalex_work_metadata.py` · writes `none` · network `true` hosts `api.openalex.org` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: When explicitly invoked, sends one HTTPS GET to api.openalex.org/works. It transmits only the caller-supplied DOI values in a DOI filter; no email, API key, title, source ID, credential, arbitrary host, or other external action is sent., Selects id, doi, display_name, publication_year, type, is_retracted, open_access, locations, and best_oa_location; keeps the raw body in memory long enough to parse and hash, then emits no raw response or article content and writes no repository files., Projects no more than 25 location records per Work. It returns only bounded source fields and HTTPS URLs without embedded credentials or nonstandard ports; unsafe URL fields are omitted. It does not follow, resolve, or request any returned URL., Does not follow redirects or retry; uses a 5-second socket timeout, 7-second response-body budget, 8 KiB bounded reads, and a 256 KiB response cap. HTTP, rate-limit, malformed, oversized, ambiguous, non-exact, and network-error outcomes remain explicit., A prior v0.1 request and the successful v0.2 same-task request each reported one API credit; the successful v0.2 response had meta.cost=null. A separate earlier v0.2 invocation returned NETWORK_ERROR. These observations do not establish stable cost or latency; no API key or paid account was used..
- Dependencies: none declared; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library; no third-party runtime dependency.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · One bounded API request per invocation. One prior v0.1 and one successful v0.2 request each reported one API credit, while v0.2 meta.cost was null; a separate v0.2 request failed with NETWORK_ERROR. These single observations do not establish stable cost, latency, or paid-account cost.
- Alternatives: `research.verify_crossref_metadata` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Crossref compares caller-supplied expected title/year to Crossref fields; OpenAlex returns its Work ID and Retraction Watch-derived signal. One paired DOI matched on title/year but cannot establish provider quality or replacement value.; `research.resolve_pubmed_identifiers` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The PubMed/PMC resolver maps DOI identifiers to NCBI IDs; this tool returns OpenAlex Work identity and selected OpenAlex metadata.; `ignition.scripts.external-research.openalex_client.fetch_by_doi` (EXISTING_BROADER_LIBRARY_FUNCTION_NOT_TOOLBOX_EQUIVALENT): The existing general client supports broader DOI Work metadata and abstract/search workflows. This wrapper adds an opt-in fixed-host contract, exact-primary-DOI matching, bounded location projection, response hashes, and explicit ambiguity. The implementations have distinct boundaries; no comparison establishes overall superiority or retirement.; `research.resolve_europe_pmc_fulltext_locations` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Europe PMC returns its own typed-identifier fullTextUrlList and availability codes; OpenAlex returns Work-level locations and an open_access summary. One same-DOI comparison observed eight OpenAlex location records and three Europe PMC entries, but these provider-specific sets are not comparable as coverage measures and establish no quality or replacement ranking..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Provide the optional router a constrained OpenAlex-only DOI lookup contract with exact-primary-DOI matching, selected metadata, response hash, and reported usage without exposing abstracts or arbitrary query URLs.; Return OpenAlex Work IDs and provider-reported location/open_access fields when those source-specific outputs are explicitly requested; Europe PMC remains a separate provider-specific fullTextUrlList and availability-code path, and the general OpenAlex client remains the wider search/abstract path..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · One same-DOI live location comparison between OpenAlex v0.2 and Europe PMC v0.2. OpenAlex returned eight Work-level location projections and an open_access summary; Europe PMC returned three fullTextUrlList entries with its availability codes and document styles. This supports distinct provider-specific outputs for this DOI only, not broad location coverage, comparative quality, latency, cost, URL validity, or legal reuse. One paired DOI does not establish completeness, broad reliability, quality, stable cost, or replacement..

### `research.resolve_europe_pmc_fulltext_locations` · Europe PMC 全文位置定位器 / Europe PMC Full-text Location Locator

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.2.0` · license `Wrapper BUSL-1.1; Europe PMC Web Services terms and applicable provider/data licensing terms apply. Provider-reported location and license metadata are not rights determinations. API documentation: https://europepmc.org/RestfulWebService.` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `3e3e5f2c04721e8a7a3277404f4bb077f70d2efe`.
- Can do: Resolve up to five exact DOI, PMID, or PMCID values against Europe PMC core-search results and return provider-reported fullTextUrlList metadata without requesting article URLs or assessing access rights.
- Good for: A task already has one to five public DOI, PMID, or PMCID values and needs the provider-reported Europe PMC fullTextUrlList projection for exact identifier records.; Returning bounded source identifiers, URL metadata, response hashes, retrieval timestamps, and explicit no-match or ambiguous-match states without article content.; Checking whether this provider reports candidate locations before a separately authorized retrieval or rights review performed by another workflow.
- Not for: Topic-to-literature discovery when no DOI is known; use a suitable bounded topic search instead.; Downloading, parsing, summarizing, quoting, or checking the returned article URLs or article text.; Verifying that a location works, that open access is legally reusable, that a license is valid, or that a source is authentic.; Establishing article quality, retraction status, clinical advice, or scientific support for any claim.
- Use only if: The caller supplies one to five unique bare DOI, PMID, or PMCID values and exact Europe PMC location metadata is needed.; The candidate is explicitly selected for this task and the caller separately permits sending those public identifiers to www.ebi.ac.uk.; The actual CLI invocation includes --allow-public-network; routing remains a proposal and does not authorize network access.; The identifiers contain no confidential, patient-identifying, or otherwise sensitive information that should not be sent to a public service.
- Do not use when: No persistent identifier is known, the task needs general topic discovery, or a checked-out local source already satisfies the request.; Public network access or disclosure of the identifier to Europe PMC is not allowed.; The task requires the article itself, a verified working URL, a license determination, a rights decision, or scientific appraisal.; The caller requests unrestricted or exhaustive location discovery beyond the provider response bounded to five records per identifier.
- Inputs → outputs: Legacy UTF-8 JSON stdin object with exactly one key dois and an array of 1-5 unique bare DOI strings (each at most 128 characters), or typed UTF-8 JSON stdin object with exactly one key identifiers and an array of 1-5 unique {type,value} objects where type is DOI, PMID, or PMCID; request maximum 4096 bytes. → Input-ordered per-identifier acquisition and exact-match state, provider hit count, and at most five exact-identifier-matched provider records., Each row preserves requested_identifier type/value and keeps requested_doi populated only for legacy/typed DOI requests; records include provider source/id/PMID/PMCID/DOI, isOpenAccess claim, safe HTTPS fullTextUrlList fields, and omitted-unsafe count., Provider endpoint, UTC retrieval timestamp, query and response SHA-256 values, source-code SHA-256, top-level status, claim ceiling, and valid/acquired response counts; no title, abstract, raw response, or article content.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_FIXED_HOST_NETWORK_QUERY` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_europe_pmc_fulltext_locator.py` · writes `none` · network `true` hosts `www.ebi.ac.uk` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: One explicit invocation sends one HTTPS GET per identifier, at most five requests total, to the fixed www.ebi.ac.uk Europe PMC Search endpoint: DOI:<value>, EXT_ID:<value> for PMID, or PMCID:<value>, with format=json, resultType=core, and pageSize=5. Caller-supplied identifiers may appear in provider-side access logs., Returned fullTextUrlList links are output metadata only: the adapter never requests them, follows redirects, downloads content, or checks live availability or license., Uses no retries, secrets, API key, billing account, file write, repository mutation, or external action; only a bounded JSON receipt is written to stdout., Each API request has a five-second socket timeout, each response body has a seven-second time budget and a 512 KiB cap, and no response contains more than five provider records..
- Dependencies: none declared; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · No service availability or stable latency/cost comparison is established; one explicit invocation performs at most five bounded fixed-host API requests.
- Alternatives: `research.search_europe_pmc_query` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The existing lite topic/query adapter remains useful for discovering records from public text; same PMID evidence shows it can return the record identity but omits fullTextUrlList, while core results expose provider-reported locations.; `research.lookup_openalex_work_metadata` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): OpenAlex returns bounded Work-level locations and an open_access summary for DOI inputs, with OpenAlex source/version/license fields; it does not provide Europe PMC's provider-specific fullTextUrlList and availability codes. One same-DOI comparison supports task-specific coexistence, not broad coverage, quality, cost, or replacement conclusions.; `research.resolve_pubmed_identifiers` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The resolver remains useful when PMID/PMCID/DOI cross-reference mapping is itself required. For an already-known PMID or PMCID and a Europe PMC location task, the direct typed locator can skip that extra lookup stage; this does not establish the resolver is replaceable..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: For already-known public DOI, PMID, or PMCID values, retrieve exact Europe PMC provider-reported fullTextUrlList metadata while leaving returned article URLs untouched and preserving rights and availability uncertainty..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · One DOI same-provider core-versus-lite comparison showed that lite omitted fullTextUrlList while core returned three locations. A second live same-PMID comparison returned the same MED identity from both adapters; only core supplied fullTextUrlList. A separate same-DOI cross-provider comparison paired Europe PMC v0.2 and OpenAlex v0.2 and observed distinct provider-specific location schemas. The typed-identifier location use remains distinct; none of these samples establishes broad superiority, stable latency/cost, rights, provider completeness, or scientific quality. Comparators: research.search_europe_pmc_query, research.resolve_pubmed_identifiers, research.lookup_openalex_work_metadata; implementation refs: none recorded; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-europe-pmc-fulltext-locator-evidence-r0.json, ignition/tools/toolbox_europe_pmc_query_search.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_europe_pmc_query_search.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_openalex_work_metadata.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_openalex_work_metadata.py, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-openalex-locations-c1-evidence-r0.json; findings: For the same DOI, lite and core returned MED record id 19404397; the lite projection omitted fullTextUrlList, while core supplied three provider-reported locations. The provider response said isOpenAccess=Y but all location license fields were null; no permission inference follows.; For the same PMID 19404397, the existing lite query returned one exact MED record identity without fullTextUrlList; the typed core locator returned the same identity and three provider-reported locations using one request in each arm. A separate typed PMCID request for PMC2671458 returned that same exact record and three locations.; The topic-search adapter retains its discovery use case. The PubMed identifier resolver remains useful for cross-reference mapping, but a known PMID/PMCID location task can skip that extra step. No existing tool is removed, made Current, or enabled for default routing.; For the same DOI, OpenAlex v0.2 returned eight Work-level location records and an OpenAlex open_access summary; Europe PMC core returned three fullTextUrlList entries with Europe PMC availability codes. The different provider schemas and counts do not measure comparative completeness. Neither response validates returned URLs, licenses, or article content..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · Retain as an opt-in exact persistent-identifier location metadata adapter. Current topic search and identifier cross-reference tools have distinct functions; the extension does not establish a replacement or retirement case.
- Migration/rollback: The legacy DOI request shape remains accepted. Use typed PMID/PMCID inputs when those identifiers are already known; keep the identifier resolver for cross-reference work and the topic-search adapter for discovery. / If typed identifier lookups fail exact-match or compatibility checks, revert to the 0.1.0 DOI-only source and generated card; preserve the original DOI smoke and all failed typed-identifier evidence.; If later same-task evidence proves the overall candidate adds no useful output, remove its optional card and generated projection and retire unneeded runtime code while preserving historical evidence..
- Invocation example: `python3 -B ignition/tools/toolbox_europe_pmc_fulltext_locator.py --stdin --allow-public-network`
- Fallback: If Europe PMC is unavailable, returns no exact match, or lists no usable URL, preserve that status without inferring absence. Use another explicitly authorized provider or manual lookup only under the caller's separate network and rights instructions; do not fetch returned locations through this tool.
- Evidence: `REAL_TASK_OUTCOME` · source refs `ignition/tools/toolbox_europe_pmc_fulltext_locator.py` (BOUNDED_FIXED_HOST_TYPED_IDENTIFIER_CLI), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_europe_pmc_fulltext_locator.py` (TYPED_IDENTIFIER_MATCH_SCHEMA_AND_NEGATIVE_CONTROLS), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_routing.py` (DEFAULT_OFF_EXPLICIT_HOST_AND_TYPED_ROUTE_TEST), `ignition/schemas/operations/toolbox-europe-pmc-fulltext-locator-r0.schema.json` (MACHINE_INPUT_OUTPUT_CONTRACT), `ignition/docs/operations/toolbox-europe-pmc-fulltext-locator.md` (CONTRACT_AND_PRIMARY_PROVIDER_DOCUMENTATION), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-europe-pmc-fulltext-locator-evidence-r0.json` (LIVE_TOOL_SMOKE_AND_C1_SAME_TASK_COMPARISON), `ignition/tools/toolbox_europe_pmc_query_search.py` (C1_SAME_PROVIDER_LITE_COMPARATOR), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_europe_pmc_query_search.py` (C1_LITE_COMPARATOR_CONTRACT), `ignition/tools/toolbox_openalex_work_metadata.py` (C1_KNOWN_DOI_METADATA_SCOPE_COMPARATOR), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_openalex_work_metadata.py` (C1_OPENALEX_CONTRACT), `ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py` (C1_IDENTIFIER_SCOPE_COMPARATOR), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py` (C1_IDENTIFIER_COMPARATOR_CONTRACT), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-openalex-locations-c1-evidence-r0.json` (C1_CROSS_PROVIDER_SAME_TASK_EVIDENCE); Only the fixed Europe PMC HTTPS host is requested; redirects, rate limits, non-JSON responses, timeout, oversized bodies, malformed provider JSON, duplicate provider keys, and invalid hit counts remain explicit.; Returned URLs are never requested; non-HTTPS URLs, credentials, fragments, invalid ports, control characters, and malformed values are omitted and counted.; Abstracts, titles, raw provider payloads, article content, inferred rights, and execution authorization are never returned..
- Route default: `false` · conditions: Remain excluded unless the caller explicitly allowlists research.resolve_europe_pmc_fulltext_locations for a known DOI, PMID, or PMCID fullTextUrlList metadata task.; The typed route profile must separately list the exact www.ebi.ac.uk network host; route selection is not execution permission.; Every actual CLI call requires --allow-public-network and a per-task decision that the supplied public identifier may be sent to Europe PMC..
- Claim ceiling: Reports exact DOI, PMID, or PMCID Europe PMC records and provider-reported fullTextUrlList fields at retrieval time only. It does not request or inspect article URLs/content, verify link availability or licensing, establish lawful reuse, authenticate the source, assess article status, or establish scientific support.

### `research.resolve_pubmed_identifiers` · PubMed/PMC DOI 标识符解析器 / PubMed/PMC DOI identifier resolver

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.1` · license `Wrapper BUSL-1.1; NCBI PMC ID Converter service terms apply, and no license for underlying record metadata is asserted. API documentation: https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/tools/idconv/.` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `566c6d84149b11b0bbb7a9417094a146b8601f80`.
- Can do: Resolve caller-supplied bare DOIs to matching PMID and PMCID identifiers through the fixed PMC ID Converter API, normalizing NCBI's numeric JSON PMID representation to a bounded decimal string.
- Good for: Mapping caller-supplied DOI identifiers to PubMed PMID and PMC PMCID values when those identifiers are returned by NCBI's ID Converter.; Retaining per-DOI source response hashes, HTTP outcomes, and explicit no-match results.; Adding an NCBI/PMC identifier lookup alongside a separately requested Crossref DOI metadata comparison.; Normalizing a positive bounded integer PMID returned by NCBI while preserving the legacy decimal-string form.
- Not for: Retrieving article full text, abstracts, publisher pages, or arbitrary URLs.; Establishing that a paper is authentic, scientifically correct, retracted, or supports a claim.; Treating a missing PMID or PMCID as proof that a DOI or article does not exist.; Adding or correcting repository records or registering scientific sources.
- Use only if: The caller provides one to ten unique bare DOI identifiers and explicitly permits sending those DOI identifiers to the public API for this invocation.; The caller invokes the CLI with --allow-public-network.; The exact fixed host pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov is allowed by the actual invocation environment; route context alone is not authority.; The PMC ID Converter endpoint is reachable and returns a supported JSON response.
- Do not use when: A DOI or its presence in task context is confidential or must not be sent to a public service.; Network access is unavailable, disallowed, or not explicitly approved for this invocation.; The task requires article-content review, source-authenticity review, retraction adjudication, or scientific assessment.
- Inputs → outputs: JSON stdin object with one to ten unique bare DOI identifiers → input-ordered PubMed/PMC identifier resolution rows, per-response SHA-256 and explicit HTTP or network outcome summary
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_FIXED_HOST_NETWORK_QUERY` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py` · writes `none` · network `true` hosts `pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: When explicitly invoked with --allow-public-network, sends at most ten HTTPS GET requests to the fixed pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/tools/idconv/api/v1/articles/ endpoint; only the DOI identifier and response-format parameter are sent., Uses no redirects, secrets, API key, billing, or arbitrary URL. Returns a compact JSON receipt on stdout and validation errors on stderr; does not create or modify repository files., Applies a five-second socket timeout, seven-second response-body budget checked between and after bounded 8 KiB reads, a 128 KiB response cap, and a serial 0.34-second inter-request delay; no retries are performed..
- Dependencies: none declared; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · No comparable latency, bandwidth, service-availability, or compute-cost profile has been measured; the wrapper has a maximum of ten serial fixed-host lookups and bounded response handling. R1 recorded one process wall/child-CPU observation (622.937/71.600 ms) for one public request; billed cost, bandwidth and latency distribution remain UNKNOWN.
- Alternatives: `research.verify_crossref_metadata` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Crossref compares caller-supplied DOI title/year expectations with Crossref metadata; this tool maps DOI identifiers to NCBI PMID/PMCID values and does not verify title or year..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Resolve one to ten DOI identifiers to NCBI-returned PMID/PMCID values without requesting PubMed update relations.; Provide the first DOI-to-PMID/PMCID stage for the optional single-DOI update-audit composition..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · One live DOI pair exposed the NCBI numeric-PMID response form and demonstrated its use as the identifier stage in a raw-update-link workflow. The candidate remains separately useful for DOI-only identifier lookup. Comparators: research.verify_crossref_metadata, research.audit_pubmed_updates_from_doi; implementation refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_doi_update_audit.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_update_link_reader.py; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-c1-evidence-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-real-task-r0.json, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py; findings: A live PMC ID Converter response encoded PMID as an integer; v0.1.0 dropped it. v0.1.1 normalizes bounded positive integers while retaining string inputs and rejecting booleans, zero, negatives, and over-limit values.; The resolver still covers DOI-to-PMID/PMCID lookup without a PubMed relation request; the composition proceeds to the second stage only when an exact PMID is returned.; One DOI comparison does not establish provider completeness, broad reliability, cost, or retirement of either adapter..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · Optional/default-off DOI identifier lookup remains useful independently; the same-task composition extends this capability but does not cover DOI-only lookup tasks.
- Migration/rollback: No automatic migration is inferred; preserve this exact input/output contract until a same-task comparison supports specialization or replacement.; Version 0.1.1 adds support for the observed positive integer PMID JSON form and preserves decimal-string inputs; downstream output remains a normalized string and the resolver remains optional/default-off.; Do not migrate DOI-only identifier lookup callers to the composition; they would incur an unnecessary second public request when a PMID is present. / Remove the candidate entry and wrapper from the isolated toolbox branch, regenerate the identity and human-directory projections, and preserve prior commits and evidence.; If integer normalization causes an incompatible consumer result, revert the bounded numeric branch in _normalized_identifier, restore the prior wrapper version and candidate card, rerun focused tests, and preserve the provider-response evidence.; If version 0.1.1 numeric normalization is incompatible, restore resolver 0.1.0 and its card while retaining the exact provider-shape evidence..
- Invocation example: `python3 -B ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py --stdin --allow-public-network`
- Fallback: If live network use is not approved or fails, preserve the explicit NOT_RUN or acquisition failure and use the official PubMed/PMC interface manually only when the caller separately authorizes that lookup; never infer an absent record from an unavailable response.
- Evidence: `REAL_TASK_OUTCOME` · source refs `ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py` (BOUNDED_FIXED_HOST_CLI), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py` (FOCUSED_MOCKED_NETWORK_AND_NEGATIVE_CONTROLS), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-real-task-route-evidence-r0.json` (REAL_TASK_ROUTE_AND_OUTCOME_EVIDENCE), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-real-task-route-evidence-r1.json` (R1_REPEAT_REAL_ROUTE_AND_MEASURED_SINGLE_RUN_OUTCOME), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-c1-evidence-r0.json` (C1_MATCHED_COMPOSITION_COMPARISON_AND_INTEGER_ID_FINDING), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-real-task-r0.json` (PRESERVED_PRE_FIX_NO_EXACT_PMID_OUTCOME), `ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_doi_update_audit.py` (C1_COMPOSED_ROUTE_IMPLEMENTATION_COMPARATOR), `ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_update_link_reader.py` (C1_EXACT_PMID_STAGE_IMPLEMENTATION_COMPARATOR); focused checks [ignition/tests/test_toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py](tests/test_toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py); negative controls: Missing --allow-public-network makes no request and emits no result.; Reject URLs, malformed or duplicate DOI identifiers, duplicate JSON keys, and batches above ten before network access.; Only pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov is contacted; redirects, non-JSON, oversized responses, HTTP errors, timeout, malformed JSON, mismatched records, and invalid identifiers remain explicit outcomes.; A no-match response yields no invented PMID/PMCID; no repository files are written..
- Route default: `false` · conditions: Remain excluded unless the candidate ID is explicitly allowlisted and the typed route profile explicitly lists pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov in allowed_network_hosts.; A route proposal has execution_authorized=false and does not validate the caller authority behind that host list.; Every actual CLI invocation separately requires --allow-public-network and a task-specific decision to transmit the supplied DOI identifiers..
- Claim ceiling: DOI-to-PMID/PMCID identifier lookup from the live PMC ID Converter response only; response hashes bind received bytes, not source authenticity, article content, bibliographic accuracy, scientific truth, retraction status, or complete PubMed/PMC coverage.

### `research.search_europe_pmc_query` · Europe PMC 主题检索 / Europe PMC topic search

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `Wrapper BUSL-1.1; Europe PMC Web Services terms and applicable provider/data licensing terms apply, and this wrapper asserts no license over returned metadata. API documentation: https://europepmc.org/RestfulWebService.` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `bc2822fa0ac1d9519ce1c6cc6393d211d2c741b0`.
- Can do: Search one caller-supplied public query against Europe PMC and return at most ten provider-ranked lite metadata rows with source identifiers and response evidence.
- Good for: Discovering a small set of literature metadata records from a caller-supplied public scientific topic when no DOI or PMID is known.; Returning the provider-reported source and identifiers alongside selected title, author, journal and year fields for caller-directed follow-up.; Retaining the exact query hash, response hash, retrieval time, provider hit count and bounded returned count without echoing the query.
- Not for: Exhaustive systematic-review searches, guaranteed complete recall, or treating the provider result order as a scientific quality ranking.; Retrieving or evaluating abstracts, full text, clinical advice, source authenticity, retraction status, article quality or scientific support for a claim.; Replacing exact-identifier tasks: known DOI-to-PMID/PMCID resolution, known PMID Comments/Corrections inspection, Crossref/OpenAlex DOI metadata lookup, or arXiv-ID metadata lookup.
- Use only if: The caller supplies one public, non-confidential query of at most 512 characters and a result limit from one through ten.; The caller separately chooses this experimental candidate and explicitly invokes the CLI with --allow-public-network.; The caller's actual invocation context explicitly permits sending this query to www.ebi.ac.uk; a route profile's host list is not authority.; Checked-out local source navigation or an existing article-cluster brief does not already satisfy the task; the caller specifically needs external topic-to-record discovery.; A bounded metadata discovery result is sufficient for the task and provider-side query logging is acceptable.
- Do not use when: The query contains confidential, patient-identifying, personal-health or otherwise sensitive material that should not be sent to a public service.; Network use is not permitted, a public query is not appropriate, or an exhaustive independently reproducible search is required.; The caller already has exact identifiers and only needs DOI-to-PMID/PMCID mapping, PubMed update-link inspection, Crossref/OpenAlex metadata lookup, or arXiv-ID metadata lookup.; The checked-out raw-evidence navigator or declared article-cluster source brief already supplies the evidence needed for the task.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON stdin object with exactly a public query string (1-512 characters) and integer max_results (1-10) → at most ten Europe PMC provider-ranked lite publication metadata rows with source, provider ID, DOI/PMID/PMCID when present, title, author string, journal and publication year, query SHA-256, response SHA-256, retrieval timestamp, provider hit count, returned count, HTTP and acquisition status, and bounded claim ceiling
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_FIXED_HOST_NETWORK_QUERY` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_europe_pmc_query_search.py` · writes `none` · network `true` hosts `www.ebi.ac.uk` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: One explicit invocation sends one HTTPS GET to the fixed www.ebi.ac.uk Europe PMC search endpoint, including the caller's URL-encoded query, format=json, resultType=lite and pageSize; provider-side access logging is outside this wrapper's control., Uses no redirects, retries, API key, secret, billing account, file write or repository mutation; returns selected metadata and a bounded JSON receipt on stdout., Applies a five-second socket timeout, seven-second response-body budget, 512 KiB response cap, one-request maximum and ten-record maximum..
- Dependencies: none declared; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · No service availability or latency commitment and no comparable cost profile have been established; this wrapper performs at most one fixed-host request and bounds response handling.
- Alternatives: `research.verify_crossref_metadata` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Crossref compares caller-supplied exact DOI title/year expectations to one provider record; it cannot discover candidate records from a topic query.; `research.lookup_openalex_work_metadata` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): OpenAlex requires caller-supplied DOI identifiers and returns a selected Work projection; this search discovers candidate records from text and does not replace exact DOI lookup.; `research.resolve_pubmed_identifiers` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The identifier resolver requires caller-supplied DOI values and maps them to returned PMID/PMCID values; this search discovers candidate records from a text query and does not resolve or verify a caller-supplied DOI.; `research.inspect_pubmed_update_links` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The PubMed update-link reader requires exact known PMIDs and reports raw Comments/Corrections relations; this search discovers candidate metadata and does not adjudicate update status.; `research.lookup_arxiv_paper_metadata` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The arXiv adapter requires one exact modern arXiv ID and returns that provider record; it does not perform topic-to-record discovery.; `research.compare_publication_metadata_snapshots` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The local comparator consumes already-collected Crossref and OpenAlex results for one DOI and performs no retrieval; it cannot discover records from a query.; `knowledge.cluster_source_brief` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The local source brief requires an already-declared article-cluster ID and reads the checked-out asset-card corpus; it cannot discover external records from a public text query.; `knowledge.navigate_raw_evidence` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The local navigator searches the checked-out Knowledge index and returns bounded current raw source lines; it does not query Europe PMC or return provider publication metadata for arbitrary external topics..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Find a bounded set of Europe PMC records from a public topic query when the checked-out source corpus is insufficient and no DOI, PMID or arXiv identifier is known, while preserving the returned source identifier for follow-up..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · Compared declared input/output contracts and execution scopes of all existing toolbox publication metadata retrieval adapters plus the two directly relevant local source-discovery alternatives; same-task search quality, completeness, availability and cost comparisons remain unmeasured. Comparators: research.verify_crossref_metadata, research.lookup_openalex_work_metadata, research.resolve_pubmed_identifiers, research.inspect_pubmed_update_links, research.lookup_arxiv_paper_metadata, research.compare_publication_metadata_snapshots, knowledge.cluster_source_brief, knowledge.navigate_raw_evidence; implementation refs: none recorded; refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_crossref_metadata_verifier.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_crossref_metadata_verifier.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_openalex_work_metadata.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_openalex_work_metadata.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_update_link_reader.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_arxiv_metadata_lookup.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_arxiv_metadata_lookup.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_compare_publication_metadata.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_publication_metadata_comparison.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_pubmed_update_link_reader.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_routing.py, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-europe-pmc-query-search-evidence-r0.json, ignition/tools/toolbox_cluster_source_brief.py, ignition/tests/test_cluster_source_brief_tool.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_raw_evidence_navigator.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_raw_evidence_navigator.py; findings: The new tool accepts a public text query and returns bounded provider metadata. Crossref and OpenAlex adapters require known DOIs, the PubMed resolver requires a known DOI, the update reader requires a known PMID, and the arXiv adapter requires a known arXiv ID; each returns a different exact-identifier or record-level output.; The local Crossref/OpenAlex comparator consumes two prior adapter results for one DOI and performs no source retrieval. None of these declared contracts covers query-to-record discovery, and the search does not cover their known-identifier or pairwise-comparison outputs.; The route experiment selects only the query-search candidate for its declared query-to-metadata task and rejects the DOI resolver as an input-type mismatch, even when both candidates are explicitly offered.; No paired evidence establishes superior relevance, recall, latency, cost or scientific quality; those comparisons remain unknown and do not support retiring any existing tool.; The local cluster brief needs a declared cluster ID, while the raw-evidence navigator searches only the checked-out Knowledge index and returns source lines. These local tools remain preferred when their bounded corpus already satisfies the task; neither discovers external topic records..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · Retain as a specialized experimental topic-to-record discovery operation; its query input and bounded metadata output do not cover known-identifier retrieval, local source navigation, or local paired-result comparison contracts.
- Migration/rollback: No existing tool or Current operation is migrated. When the caller has a known DOI, PMID or arXiv ID and needs its source-specific output, retain the corresponding exact-identifier adapter; when comparing already-collected Crossref/OpenAlex results, retain the local comparator. Use this candidate only for public-query discovery. / If later evidence supports retirement, remove this candidate from the available set and delete unneeded runtime code, then regenerate identity projections; preserve this historical query, response and route evidence without rewriting it..
- Invocation example: `python3 -B ignition/tools/toolbox_europe_pmc_query_search.py --stdin --allow-public-network`

### `research.validate_evidence_program_snapshot` · 科研证据计划快照校验器 / Evidence Program snapshot validator

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `toolbox-wrapper-0.1.0; upstream Task103 Evidence Program validator` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `b1abb127207333b53472b48be3fdc7d81b732f1c`.
- Can do: Run the existing standard-library Evidence Program validator against the fixed checked-out evidence-program tree and return a compact summary plus a source-tree digest.
- Good for: Check the fixed repository Evidence Program schemas, recorded preregistration-order metadata, source provenance fields, post-hoc threshold equality, and metric leakage constraints.; Get a compact check/failure count and SHA-256 digest for the entire fixed Evidence Program input tree.
- Not for: Running Crossref, OpenAlex, model, browser, search, or other network queries; the source-acquisition runner is a separate tool with different permissions.; Authenticating source origins, proving every file was committed as claimed, verifying scientific content, deciding truth, or recording independent/human acceptance.; Editing manifests, registrations, run records, thresholds, or the evidence-program tree.
- Use only if: The caller wants to validate the fixed `ignition/evidence-program/` snapshot in the currently checked-out Formal repository.; Python >=3.9 and the local Git CLI are available; Git is used only for repository-root discovery and preregistration ancestor checks.; The caller understands that the result validates recorded structure and consistency only, and that checkout-level Git metadata is read-only input.
- Do not use when: The task asks to fetch or refresh any evidence source; use a separately authorized acquisition workflow with network and output-path controls.; The task requires scientific adjudication, primary-source support review, authorship/authenticity proof, or epistemic acceptance.; The tool router must authorize an exact Git metadata read path; the current route planner cannot represent the containing checkout's `.git` metadata and will fail closed.
- Inputs → outputs: fixed checked-out ignition/evidence-program snapshot plus containing checkout Git metadata for read-only ancestry checks → compact validator status, check count, failure count, and bounded failure details, SHA-256 digest and file count for the complete checked-out Evidence Program tree, validator source SHA-256 and local Git root identity
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_FIXED_TREE_AND_LOCAL_GIT_METADATA` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/evidence-program/, containing checkout Git metadata (read-only; `git rev-parse` and `git merge-base --is-ancestor` only)` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Writes one compact JSON summary to stdout; no repository files are created or changed., Runs the validator and fixed-argument local Git read commands as child processes; never invokes a shell or a remote Git operation..
- Dependencies: ignition/evidence-program/tools/validate_evidence_program.py, ignition/evidence-program/; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library and local Git CLI; no third-party runtime packages.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · The fixed tree has 31 files and 499 observed checks in the current run, but no calibrated latency or memory profile has been established.
- Alternatives: `ignition.evidence_program.validate_evidence_program.py` (EQUIVALENT_VALIDATION_DIFFERENT_INTERFACE): The upstream validator exposes a configurable root and a verbose per-check log; this wrapper fixes the repository root and summarizes the same validator run..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Validate only the fixed checked-out Evidence Program tree and emit a bounded machine-readable receipt that binds the tree and validator source hashes, without exposing a caller-selected root; use the direct validator when detailed per-check logs or another declared root are needed..
- Comparison evidence: `COMPARISON_UNKNOWN` · One paired run on the same Formal candidate snapshot and Evidence Program tree compared the fixed-root JSON wrapper with its underlying direct validator; this is interface/receipt evidence, not an independent correctness or cost benchmark. Comparators: none registered; implementation refs: none recorded; refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_validate_evidence_program.py, ignition/evidence-program/tools/validate_evidence_program.py, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-c1-evidence-program-validator-comparison-r0.json, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_validate_evidence_program.py, ignition/evidence-program/tests/test_evidence_program.py; findings: Both commands invoked identical underlying validator source bytes on the same 31-file tree and returned exit 0 with 499 checks and zero failures.; The wrapper adds a fixed repository root, bounded JSON receipt, Evidence Program tree digest, and validator-source hash; the direct validator retains verbose per-check output and arbitrary declared-root support.; This one matched run compares a toolbox candidate with an upstream script interface, not two registered toolbox tools; no paired task utility, reliability, or cost evidence establishes a retention advantage or replacement. Keep the candidate default-off and retain COMPARISON_UNKNOWN..
- Deprecation/retirement: `COMPARISON_UNKNOWN` · One exact-snapshot parity comparison observed different interfaces, but the direct validator is not a registered toolbox identity and no paired task utility, reliability, or cost evidence supports a retention or replacement decision; keep the candidate default-off and review again if a real consumer gap appears.
- Migration/rollback: No automatic migration is inferred; preserve this exact input/output contract until a same-task comparison supports specialization or replacement. / Remove the candidate entry and wrapper from the isolated toolbox branch, regenerate the identity and human-directory projections, and preserve prior commits and evidence..

### `research.verify_crossref_metadata` · Crossref 元数据核验器 / Crossref metadata verifier

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.3.0` · license `Wrapper BUSL-1.1; Crossref bibliographic metadata reuse basis documented at https://www.crossref.org/documentation/retrieve-metadata/.` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; Task 103 Crossref pilot supplies reused normalization helpers, not Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `3e8f0511edbe1e3ff1c80af29ff7ff19d8ed503c`.
- Can do: Compare caller-supplied DOI title/year with Crossref Work metadata and optionally project bounded update relations and deposited references from the same response.
- Good for: Comparing caller-supplied expected DOI title and year with the live Crossref metadata record.; Retaining per-record HTTP outcome, response SHA-256, observed fields, and explicit mismatch or network-failure status.; Checking public bibliographic metadata for a bounded batch without retrieving article content.; Inspecting Crossref-deposited update-to, updated-by, and typed related-item identifiers alongside title/year comparison without making a second request or following related identifiers.; Retrieving up to 25 Crossref-deposited reference citation entries in the same Work response for a known DOI, with explicit field status, provider count, omitted rows, invalid entries, and truncation.
- Not for: Retrieving article full text, abstracts, publisher pages, or arbitrary URLs.; Establishing that a paper is authentic, scientifically correct, or supports a claim.; Treating missing or stale Crossref fields as proof that a source does not exist.; Adding or correcting records in a repository registry.; Treating absent or empty provider relation fields as proof that no correction, update, or related item exists.; Establishing a complete or authoritative bibliography; Crossref reference deposits are optional and may be partial.; Resolving referenced works, following URLs, verifying citation correctness, or assessing scientific support.
- Use only if: The caller has explicitly approved transmission of the DOI identifiers in this invocation and passes --allow-public-network.; A route proposal may include this candidate only when allowed_network_hosts explicitly contains api.crossref.org; the proposal is not a permission grant.; The request contains one to ten unique bare DOI identifiers with caller-supplied expected title and year.; The public Crossref REST API is reachable from the declared environment.
- Do not use when: The DOI or associated task context is confidential or must not be sent to a public service.; Network access is unavailable, disallowed, or not explicitly approved for this invocation.; The task requires article-content review, source authenticity, current retraction adjudication, or scientific assessment.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON stdin object with one to ten unique DOI, expected-title, expected-year records → input-ordered Crossref metadata comparison rows, per-response SHA-256 and explicit HTTP or network outcome summary, bounded update-to, updated-by, and typed relation identifier projections with reported/omitted counts and truncation state, bounded Crossref reference citation projection (up to 25 entries per DOI) with missing/empty/malformed status, provider count, omitted count, invalid-entry count, and truncation state
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_FIXED_HOST_NETWORK_QUERY` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_crossref_metadata_verifier.py, ignition/evidence-program/tools/run_crossref_verification.py` · writes `none` · network `true` hosts `api.crossref.org` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: When explicitly invoked with --allow-public-network, sends at most ten HTTPS GET requests to api.crossref.org with DOI identifiers only; no user title, source_id, credential, or arbitrary URL is transmitted., Writes the JSON result to stdout and validation errors to stderr; does not create or modify repository files., Follows no redirects; uses a 5-second socket inactivity timeout, checks a 7-second response-body budget between and after 8 KiB reads, and caps responses at 256 KiB; records timeout, size, HTTP, and malformed-response failures explicitly..
- Dependencies: ignition/evidence-program/tools/run_crossref_verification.py; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library, Task103 Crossref helper source in the same checkout.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · Crossref documents public REST access without signup. The wrapper permits at most ten serial requests, applies a 5-second socket timeout and a 7-second body-read budget with bounded chunks; no comparable latency, bandwidth, service-availability, or compute-cost profile has been measured. R1 recorded one process wall/child-CPU observation (1547.352/68.352 ms) for one public request; billed cost, bandwidth and latency distribution remain UNKNOWN.
- Alternatives: `research.validate_evidence_program_snapshot` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The local validator checks the Evidence Program structure and recorded provenance but does not retrieve current Crossref metadata.; `research.lookup_openalex_work_metadata` (ALTERNATE_PROVIDER_DIFFERENT_PROVENANCE): OpenAlex can return DOI-resolved title/year plus an OpenAlex Work ID and provider-reported retraction signal. It is a different metadata source and cannot establish the content or status of the Crossref record; one paired DOI comparison is not evidence that either provider replaces the other..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Project source-deposited Crossref reference citation metadata (including optional matched DOIs and bounded citation text) from the same known-DOI Work response.; The current OpenAlex adapter does not expose referenced-work lists, and the PubMed relation tools address update/correction links rather than bibliography entries; no general coverage comparison has been run..
- Comparison evidence: `COMPARISON_UNKNOWN` · One purposively selected real DOI produced a nonempty Crossref reference projection: 40 entries reported, 25 projected, 15 omitted, and truncation=true. This is a single-record observation only; it cannot estimate deposit prevalence, coverage, completeness, correctness, or cross-provider quality. Overall comparison remains COMPARISON_UNKNOWN. Comparators: research.lookup_openalex_work_metadata, research.inspect_pubmed_update_links; implementation refs: none recorded; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-crossref-relations-evidence-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-crossref-pubmed-matched-relations-c1-evidence-r1.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-crossref-references-real-task-evidence-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-crossref-references-route-evidence-r0.json; findings: Crossref documents that reference deposits are optional; absence or emptiness does not establish that a work has no references.; The existing OpenAlex candidate returns Work identity, OA, and location projections, while the PubMed relation reader returns Comments/Corrections fields; neither current adapter returns a Crossref-deposited bibliography projection.; This feature uses fields from the same Crossref Work response and does not add a request or permission. Any single DOI result is a positive or negative record-level observation only, not a coverage or quality comparison.; For the one purposively selected record, Crossref reported 40 references and the bounded adapter projected 25, omitted 15, and marked truncation; this does not establish general coverage or completeness..
- Deprecation/retirement: `ACTIVE` · The default-off Crossref candidate has a provider-specific reference projection and distinct update/relation fields. Existing candidates do not provide the same Crossref-deposited citation rows; no same-task replacement evidence supports retiring this adapter or another tool.
- Migration/rollback: Version 0.3.0 adds crossref_reference_metadata to each successful result row while preserving the existing request contract and prior metadata fields. Consumers may ignore the additive result. / Revert the v0.3.0 reference projection to v0.2.0, regenerate identity cards and ignition/TOOLBOX.md, and rerun focused tests. Preserve real-task comparison evidence and prior source history..

### `research.verify_offline_source_snapshot_run` · 离线来源快照运行核验器 / Offline Source-Snapshot Run Verifier

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `12e99dee4b64446105f26b5cc8840d3fd78fad55`.
- Can do: Verify the exact regular-file set and per-file sizes and SHA-256 values declared by one saved E37 source-snapshot run receipt under a caller-selected external directory.
- Good for: Checking saved artifacts from the fixed E37 offline source-snapshot adapter against the unkeyed receipt in the same run directory.; Confirming receipt-listed regular-file sizes and SHA-256 values and detecting missing or unlisted files without printing artifact contents.; Reading an E37 run stored outside the current repository checkout when a caller supplies the canonical absolute directory path.
- Not for: Authenticating the receipt, capture origin, publisher, executor, prior run, or artifact provenance.; Proving semantic support, scientific truth, task correctness, or stable state after the check.; Checking FederatedResultReceipt handoffs under the repository-local ignition/ workspace contract.; Reading symlinks or special files, writing or repairing artifacts, using network/search/models, or authorizing execution or handoff.
- Use only if: The directory is a caller-selected canonical absolute path outside and not containing the repository checkout.; The run contains one ignition-source-binding-slot-run-v1 receipt from the fixed e37-source-snapshot-v1 adapter.; The caller accepts an unkeyed SHA-256 comparison as byte-consistency evidence only.
- Do not use when: Receipt authenticity, capture origin, publisher identity, execution history, or semantic/scientific review is required.; The run directory is inside or contains the repository, uses symlinks or non-regular files, or exceeds the declared artifact and byte limits.; The input is a FederatedResultReceipt or a different adapter/schema, or the request expects repair, network action, or authorization.
- Inputs → outputs: One canonical absolute filesystem path to a caller-selected external E37 run directory containing receipt.json and its receipt-listed files. → One JSON result with per-artifact byte and digest statuses, missing and unlisted paths, bounded read counts, explicit unkeyed-receipt limitations, and execution_authorized=false.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_BOUNDED_CALLER_SELECTED_EXTERNAL_RUN_TREE` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_verify_offline_source_snapshot_run.py` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Reads the selected receipt and bounded regular-file contents using descriptor-relative no-follow opens; prints one bounded JSON result to stdout and writes no files..
- Dependencies: ignition/research_workflow/slot.py; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.10 standard library., POSIX O_NOFOLLOW, O_DIRECTORY, and dir_fd support; unsupported platforms fail closed..
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · No CPU, memory, latency, or matched cost comparison has been measured; each artifact is limited to 64 MiB, total artifact reads to 128 MiB, and the receipt to 1 MiB.
- Alternatives: `collaboration.verify_handoff_artifact_files` (SPECIALIZED_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The existing verifier accepts a typed FederatedResultReceipt under a repository-local ignition/ workspace; it rejects the E37 receipt schema and cannot inspect a caller-selected external run directory..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Check byte-level integrity and exact file membership for an E37 run directory outside the repository using that run’s receipt.; Detect post-run tampering, omission, or unlisted files while keeping artifact contents out of stdout and leaving the receipt explicitly unauthenticated..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · A low-cost same-input contract comparison supplied an E37 receipt to the existing Federation byte verifier; that verifier returned INPUT_INVALID with SOURCE_RECEIPT_INVALID. The new verifier accepted the fixed E37 schema and inspected its caller-selected external run tree. This establishes a schema/root boundary only; no comparative quality, broad reliability, authenticity, or cost advantage is established. Comparators: collaboration.verify_handoff_artifact_files; implementation refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_verify_handoff_artifact_files.py; refs: ignition/tests/test_toolbox_source_snapshot_run_verifier.py, ignition/docs/operations/toolbox-verify-offline-source-snapshot-run.md; findings: The existing verifier checks a different FederatedResultReceipt contract and rejects the E37 receipt; it remains appropriate for repository-local Federation handoffs.; The new verifier covers caller-selected external E37 run trees and exact file sets, so the tools remain specialized for different declared inputs and task boundaries.; No replacement or retirement is supported; measured performance, costs, provenance, receipt authenticity, and scientific validity remain unknown..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · The contract comparison establishes a distinct E37 external-run use case and does not support replacing or retiring the existing Federation receipt verifier.
- Migration/rollback: Keep collaboration.verify_handoff_artifact_files for FederatedResultReceipt inputs under ignition/; use this candidate only for fixed E37 receipt outputs in caller-selected external directories.; No Current registry operation is migrated and no permission or scientific status is inherited by adding this optional card. / Remove this optional card, verifier, tests, route evidence, and guide from the isolated branch, then regenerate identity projections; preserve historical comparison and implementation evidence..

### `runtime.inspect_declared_pack_catalog` · 声明式 Pack 能力目录检查器 / Declared Pack catalog inspector

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `20a8685606421cc537284c2526eb18c35e759818`.
- Can do: List or show versioned Pack manifest metadata and declared capability routes from the local checkout, with source hashes; it does not load Packs or execute hooks.
- Good for: Discovering which Pack IDs and versions are declared in the checked-out repository.; Inspecting the object types, declared permissions, lifecycle policy, and capability routes of one Pack.; Checking manifest source hashes before planning a Pack-aware task.
- Not for: Proving that a declared capability is executable or that its runtime dependencies are installed in another environment.; Loading or unloading a Pack, routing a domain action, or executing any action or planning hook.; Granting permission, Owner authority, truth, proof, or scientific acceptance.
- Use only if: The task needs local Pack metadata discovery or one manifest's declared capability routes.; The checked-out ignition/packs directory contains non-symlink manifest files and the local Agent Runtime source is available.; The caller accepts declaration-only output and does not treat route metadata as runtime execution evidence.
- Do not use when: The task requires executing a Pack hook, validating task data, or proving operational availability.; The task needs current information outside the local repository snapshot.; The requested Pack ID is missing or its manifest source is ambiguous.
- Inputs → outputs: Pack metadata list request, local Pack ID → declared Pack metadata, declared capability route list, manifest and source-tree SHA-256 references
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_REPOSITORY_SNAPSHOT` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/packs/, ignition/agent_runtime/, ignition/agent_kernel/` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Writes JSON only to stdout or error text to stderr; does not load, activate, or execute any Pack hook and creates no repository files..
- Dependencies: ignition/agent_runtime/, ignition/agent_kernel/, ignition/packs/; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · The command imports the local Agent Runtime package and hashes its source trees; no latency or memory benchmark has been established.
- Alternatives: `ignition.adaptive_toolbox_route_planner` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The route planner maps typed task requirements to registered operations; this tool inspects Pack manifest declarations and does not propose or execute a task route..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: not established.
- Comparison evidence: `COMPARISON_UNKNOWN` · Existing evidence tests this candidate in isolation; no paired same-task comparison against its mapped alternatives has been recorded for C1. Comparators: none registered; implementation refs: none recorded; refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_pack_registry_inspector.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_pack_registry_inspector.py, ignition/tests/test_pack_registry.py; findings: No replacement, unique-use, or retirement conclusion is established; preserve the current optional/default-off disposition pending a task-paired comparison where overlap matters..

### `visualization.build_archify_derived_ir` · 派生架构 IR 导出器 / Derived architecture IR exporter

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `toolbox-wrapper-0.1.0; upstream adapter IGNITION-20260831-149 Step05` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `9036e4ee41c91da8a3c6da074c5b850352ed608d`.
- Can do: Build the existing Task149 provider-neutral typed visualization IR from the checked-out canonical architecture source and include the current system-map snapshot digest as context; emit JSON without writing files or invoking a renderer.
- Good for: Produce the repository's deterministic typed component/connection/boundary IR as stdout JSON for a separately selected downstream consumer.; Inspect canonical architecture node and edge coverage together with the exact source-file digests and caller-supplied revision string.
- Not for: Rendering an image, invoking Archify, validating provider delivery, or claiming visual usefulness or visual review.; Changing canonical architecture files, repairing the separately deferred Task150 geometry delta, or publishing a derived artifact.; Establishing architecture truth, Current integration, provider admission, or resolution of the caller-supplied revision to the checked-out Git HEAD.
- Use only if: The canonical architecture, current system map, and Task149 adapter source are present in the checked-out Ignition repository and pass fixed-path safety checks.; The caller supplies an exact lowercase 40-character SHA string; the tool records that string but does not resolve or verify it against Git.; The request is at most 4 KiB and both fixed canonical JSON sources are at most 1 MiB.
- Do not use when: The user needs a rendered or visually reviewed system map, fresh provider evidence, or a Current provider integration.; The requested input requires arbitrary source paths or the caller needs the formal revision independently resolved.; The task requires the Task150 geometry repair or any aesthetic, architecture-truth, Owner, or scientific claim.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON stdin object containing exactly one formal_revision lowercase commit-SHA string → typed Archify-shaped derived visualization IR with component, connection, and boundary counts, SHA-256 refs for the wrapper, reused builder, canonical architecture source, and system-map context, provider-invoked false and caller-supplied revision-binding status

- Side effects: Writes only the IR result to stdout; does not create or overwrite repository artifacts., The renderer/provider is not imported, contacted, or invoked..
- Dependencies: ignition/tools/run_task149_archify_adapter.py, ignition/data/architecture/overall-architecture.json, ignition/data/architecture/interactive-system-map.json; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library; no third-party runtime packages.

- Deprecation/retirement: `ACTIVE` · The optional experimental entry remains available and default-off; comparative retirement status is unknown.
- Migration/rollback: No automatic migration is inferred; preserve this exact input/output contract until a same-task comparison supports specialization or replacement. / Remove the candidate entry and wrapper from the isolated toolbox branch, regenerate the identity and human-directory projections, and preserve prior commits and evidence..

### `writing.preflight_cognitive_migration_revision` · 认知迁移编辑修订预检 / Cognitive-migration editorial revision preflight

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or publication authority.` · source blob `e7179d56964b3a3b0c29b29b5776ff6a5efe4d7f`.
- Can do: Build a bounded editorial proposal by reordering caller-supplied paragraphs verbatim, checking declared excerpt-to-paragraph associations, protecting evidence/counterargument/affected-person paragraphs, and reporting nonbinding structural diagnostics.
- Good for: Producing a local proposed order for explicitly selected source paragraphs without changing their wording.; Checking that caller-declared exact excerpt strings occur in their assigned paragraphs and echoing caller-supplied source references without inspecting those sources; keeping anchored, counterargument, and affected-person paragraphs in a proposal.; Reviewing caller-declared cognitive-move variety, concept-to-anchor mapping, paragraph lengths, and repeated surface patterns as nonbinding editorial prompts.
- Not for: Generating, paraphrasing, deleting or fact-checking prose; selecting an editorial direction on the author's behalf; or publishing or accepting a work.; Verifying that a source reference is real, that an excerpt is faithfully transcribed, that a factual claim is true, or that a claim stays within its ceiling.; Treating one paragraph-count range, cognitive-move set, surface pattern, readability signal, or proposal status as a universal writing rule or quality judgment.
- Use only if: The caller supplies one UTF-8 JSON request no larger than 1,000,000 bytes with exact source paragraphs, declared anchor excerpts and references, a claim ceiling, reader-shift fields, and the desired paragraph order.; The caller accepts that caller-declared evidence, counterargument, affected-person, and source-anchor paragraphs cannot be omitted from the generated proposal.; The result is reviewed by the author for factual support, scope, voice, and suitability before any use.
- Do not use when: The task requires fresh sources, source authentication, factual or causal adjudication, or independent claim-ceiling review.; The caller expects automatic paraphrase, paragraph selection, style imitation, publication approval, or a finished article.; The source draft or editorial decision is not authorized for local processing.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON stdin containing 1–80 identified paragraphs, declared exact anchors and source-reference strings, before/after reader-shift statements, a caller-supplied claim ceiling, and an ordered selection (maximum 1,000,000 bytes) → deterministic JSON review receipt with wrapper, source-paragraph, and proposal SHA-256, declared move and concept-anchor coverage, paragraph diagnostics, and explicit unverified-source boundaries, optional proposed text formed only by joining caller-selected paragraph text verbatim in caller order; withheld if protected content is omitted
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_BOUNDED_STDIN_AND_SELF_HASH` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_cognitive_migration_revision.py` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Reads one bounded JSON request from stdin and its own regular wrapper file for SHA-256, then writes a deterministic receipt to stdout; it creates no files and does not modify the repository., When protected content is omitted, returns a blocker and no proposal text; when safe unanchored paragraphs are omitted, reports their IDs for human review..
- Dependencies: ignition/tools/toolbox_cognitive_migration_revision.py; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · No comparable latency or memory profile has been measured; input is capped at 1,000,000 bytes and processing is local deterministic text/JSON work.
- Alternatives: `writing.apply_editorial_method` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The Current registered operation describes the broader writing method; this optional CLI only checks caller annotations and reorders existing paragraphs without applying generative or editorial judgment.; `writing.validate_publication_surface` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The Current registered operation covers publication-surface validation; this CLI produces a local editorial structure proposal and does not validate publication readiness..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: For an already-written draft supplied as structured paragraphs, produce a caller-ordered verbatim reorder with excerpt-to-paragraph checks and fail-closed protection of declared evidence, counterargument, and affected-person paragraphs; this exact contract is absent from the two compared Current writing operations..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · Exact current operation input/output declarations plus one bounded synthetic CLI and route comparison; not a paired human writing-quality, cost, reliability, or real-task benchmark. Comparators: writing.apply_editorial_method, writing.validate_publication_surface; implementation refs: none recorded; refs: ignition/data/operations/ignition-operation-capability-registry-r1.json, ignition/packs/writing/manifest.json, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_cognitive_migration_revision.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_routing.py; findings: writing.apply_editorial_method declares source-bounded L6 transformation and draft/provenance outputs; this candidate never rewrites prose and cannot cover those generation uses.; writing.validate_publication_surface declares publication-surface and provenance validation; this candidate neither renders nor validates a publication surface.; Exact-contract route trials selected each Current operation for its own request; paragraph reordering was INCOMPLETE by default and selected this candidate only after explicit allowlisting, with execution_authorized=false.; The synthetic CLI returned a verbatim reorder and retained designated counterargument and affected-person paragraphs; no paired real-task quality or cost advantage is established..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · Keep as a default-off structural editing option; evidence does not support replacing either Current writing operation or promoting this candidate to ordinary default routing.
- Migration/rollback: Use only for explicit structured paragraph reordering; keep generative editing and publication-surface validation on their separately declared Current operations. / Remove this candidate entry and wrapper from the isolated toolbox branch, rebuild the identity-card and human-directory projections, and retain this comparison evidence and source refs..
- Invocation example: `python3 -B ignition/tools/toolbox_cognitive_migration_revision.py --stdin < request.json`
- Fallback: If the proposal is blocked or the tool is unavailable, preserve the full draft and reorder paragraphs manually; review all factual claims and source links against the original evidence.
- Evidence: `REAL_TOOL_TEST` · source refs `ignition/tools/toolbox_cognitive_migration_revision.py` (BOUNDED_VERBATIM_EDITORIAL_PROPOSAL_ENTRYPOINT), `ignition/docs/publication/zhiyuan-writing-method.md` (CURRENT_GENERATIVE_METHOD_BOUNDARY), `ignition/templates/publication/zhiyuan-writing-spec.md` (CURRENT_WRITING_SPEC_BOUNDARY), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_cognitive_migration_revision.py` (FOCUSED_SYNTHETIC_AND_NEGATIVE_CONTROLS), `ignition/data/operations/ignition-operation-capability-registry-r1.json` (BOUNDED_COMPARISON_EVIDENCE), `ignition/packs/writing/manifest.json` (BOUNDED_COMPARISON_EVIDENCE), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_routing.py` (BOUNDED_COMPARISON_EVIDENCE); focused checks [ignition/tests/test_toolbox_cognitive_migration_revision.py](tests/test_toolbox_cognitive_migration_revision.py), [ignition/tests/test_toolbox_routing.py](tests/test_toolbox_routing.py); negative controls: Omitting any declared evidence-anchor, counterargument, or affected-person paragraph withholds proposal text and reports every protected paragraph ID.; An anchor excerpt absent from its attached paragraph, a missing concept anchor, an unknown selected paragraph, duplicate JSON keys, invalid input, and oversized requests fail closed without a partial stdout receipt.; Repeated contrast/deepening patterns and an incomplete cognitive-move set are review advisories; phrases are not banned and declared move labels are never independently inferred from prose.; Repeated invocations on identical input produce identical JSON, and unsupported output-path options are rejected; no file, network, secret, billing, or external-action effect is exposed..
- Route default: `false` · conditions: Remain excluded unless writing.preflight_cognitive_migration_revision is explicitly allowlisted for a user-requested, source-bounded editorial structure proposal.; The caller supplies exact accepted inputs, desired outputs, and the allowed READ_ONLY_RUN mode; route planning does not execute the tool or verify user authority over the draft.; The route proposal has execution_authorized=false and does not confer publication, truth, evidence, or Owner authority..
- Claim ceiling: Deterministic editorial structure proposal over caller-supplied paragraphs only.
